   799	
   800	// Identity and head are a single snapshot from the owner of the evaluated
   801	// operation. Mutation replay's seq is historical; head is never that receipt.
   802	fn response_head(engine: &mut Engine) -> Result<(String, u64), Refusal> {
   803	    let tx = engine.store_mut().read_transaction()?;
   804	    let id: String = tx.conn().query_row("SELECT value FROM _meta WHERE key = 'store_id'", [], |row| row.get(0)).map_err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::from)?;
   805	    Ok((id, tx.next_seq()? - 1))
   806	}
   807	
   808	// @ref LLP 3000.013#6 — a replay belongs to its acknowledged store, even if
   809	// the endpoint changes between the client's probe and this transaction.
   810	fn intent_store(engine: &Engine, payload: &Json) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
   811	    let Some(expected) = payload.get("store_id").filter(|id| !id.is_null()) else { return Ok(()); };
   812	    let current = engine.store().meta("store_id")?;
   813	    if expected.as_str().is_some_and(|id| Some(id) == current.as_deref()) { return Ok(()); }
   814	    Err(Refusal::new("E_STORE_REPLACED", "store", "the intent belongs to another store; its original outcome is unknown")
   815	        .site("request.store_id").rewrite("reconcile this write with its original store before submitting new intent").guide("sync"))
   816	}
   817	
   818	fn operation_refusal(request: Request, shared: &Shared, status: u16, why: Refusal) {
   819	    let metadata = shared.read_identity();
   820	    match metadata {
   821	        Ok((store_id, head, generation)) => respond_timed(request, shared, status, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&why), "store_id": store_id, "head": head, "generation": generation })),
   822	        Err(why) => respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&why) })),
   823	    }
   824	}
   825	
   826	// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — a racing id conflict is a terminal input refusal, not a transport failure.
   827	fn mutation_error_status(refusal: &Refusal) -> u16 {
   828	    if refusal.retryable { 503 } else if matches!(refusal.code.as_str(), "E_ID_REUSED" | "E_DELIVERY_BINDING") { 200 } else { 500 }
   829	}
   830	
   831	fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
   832	    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000#93-http-admission-and-reporting — lookup, decode and execution share the admitted release.
   833	    let operation = request.url().split('?').next().is_some_and(|p| p.starts_with("/q/") || p.starts_with("/m/"));
   834	    let _scope=crate::release_requests::Scope::begin(*lock(&shared.generation));
   835	
   836	    request.set_cleanup_guard(latency::FinishOnDrop);
   837	    #[cfg(test)] test_work(&shared.request_work);
   838	    let method = request.method().clone();
   839	    let url = request.url().to_owned();
   840	    let path = url.split('?').next().unwrap_or("").to_owned();
   841	    let _evidence = dev_refusals::response::begin(shared, &path);
   842	    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — closed read allow-list precedes every handler,
   843	    // declared route, dev door and idempotency replay. Refusals carry no head.
   844	    // Read tokens and malformed credentials always enter this guard. Otherwise
   845	    // authenticated full-token handlers retain their input/refusal ordering.
   846	    // Public and dev doors must resolve any supplied credential before dispatch.
   847	    let public_door = path.starts_with("/dev/") || matches!(path.as_str(), "/seed" | "/auth/signup" | "/auth/login" | "/auth/guest")
   848	        || path == token::ENDPOINT || routes::declared(shared, &url).is_some();
   849	    if request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("authorization")
   850	        && (public_door || crate::auth::parse_bearer(h.value.as_str()).is_none_or(|token| token.starts_with("ro.")))) {
   851	        match principal_of(&request, shared) {
   852	            Ok((_, _, snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Read)) if !read_permitted(&method, &path) =>
   853	                return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied": Refusal::new("E_READ_ONLY", "auth", "this read session permits reads only; use a full session for writes").guide("read-only")})),
   854	            // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — the private-proof door keeps one indistinguishable refusal.
   855	            Err(_) if path == token::ENDPOINT => return token::denied(request),
   856	            Err(why) => return respond_timed(request, shared, if why.retryable {503} else {401}, json!({"denied": refusal_json(&why)})),
   857	            _ => {}
   858	        }
   859	    }
   860	    if path == token::ENDPOINT { return token::handle(request, shared); }
   861	    // This exact declared POST path is the only non-loopback development
   862	    // exception. Core doors, mismatched methods and query variants stay fenced.
   863	    if method == Method::Post && let Some((name, route)) = routes::declared(shared, &url) {
   864	        return routes::ingress(request, shared, &name, route);
   865	    }
   866	    let ephemeral_request = method == Method::Post && path.strip_prefix("/m/").is_some_and(|op| lock(&shared.bound).schema.operations.get(op).is_some_and(|f| f.ephemeral));
   867	    let reject = |request, status, refusal: Refusal| {
   868	        if ephemeral_request { ephemeral::failed(request,shared,None,refusal); }
   869	        else if path.starts_with("/q/") { operation_refusal(request, shared, status, refusal); }
   870	        else { respond_timed(request,shared,status,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }
   871	    };
   872	    if let Err(refusal) = dev_request(&request, shared) {
   873	        if ephemeral_request { return ephemeral::failed(request, shared, None, refusal); }
   874	        respond_with_sync(request, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }), false, None, Some(shared));
   875	        return;
   876	    }
   877	    // /dev/token has already been routed through its mutual local-owner proof.
   878	    if shared.personas && (path.starts_with("/dev/") || path == "/seed") && !shared.dev_doors() {
   879	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&dev_doors::closed())}));
   880	    }
   881	    if path.starts_with("/dev/clock") && !shared.personas {
   882	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release()) }));
   883	    }
   884	    // Discovered control requests bind to the inspected serving instance.
   885	    // A listener replaced between GET status and POST cannot receive the action.
   886	    if method==Method::Post && matches!(path.as_str(), "/dev/clock/advance"|"/dev/reset"|"/dev/latency/reset") {
   887	        match single_header(&request,"x-snapback-dev-instance") {
   888	            Ok(None)=>{},
   889	            Ok(Some(instance)) if shared.dev_identity.as_ref().is_some_and(|i| i.instance==instance)=>{},
   890	            _=>return respond_timed(request,shared,409,json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_INPUT","input","dev instance changed since discovery; rerun the command to select the live host"))})),
   891	        }
   892	    }
   893	    if (method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/latency") || (method == Method::Post && path == "/dev/latency/reset") {
   894	        return latency::endpoint(request, shared, method == Method::Post);
   895	    }
   896	    // @ref LLP 3000.000.000.000.000#3-proof-fixture-loading — development field types need no principal.
   897	    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/catalog" {
   898	        if !shared.personas { return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "fixture catalog requires dev"))})); }
   899	        let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
   900	        let tables: Vec<Json> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, table)| json!({ "name": name, "fields": table.columns.iter().filter(|(column, _)| !table.private_field(column) && !table.engine_field(column)).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>() })).collect();
   901	        drop(bound);
   902	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({ "tables": tables }));
   903	    }
   904	    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/status" {
   905	        if !shared.personas { return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "status inspection requires dev"))})); }
   906	        // No engine lock: doctor must remain usable while an adoption waits on SQLite.
   907	        let generation = *lock(&shared.generation);
   908	        let personas = lock(&shared.bound).personas.clone();
   909	        let status = lock(&shared.adoption);
   910	        let now = shared.now();
   911	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"instance":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.instance), "initialStoreId":shared.dev_identity.as_ref().map(|i| &i.initial_store_id), "pid":std::process::id(), "wrapperPid":crate::supervisor::pid(), "storeId":status.store_id, "project":shared.project, "temporary":shared.temporary, "generation":generation, "adoption":status.failure, "now":now, "clockOffsetMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "skewMs":now - snapback4_core::now_ms(), "personas":personas, "scheduling":crate::thread_priority::diagnostics(), "fileDescriptors":crate::host_limits::diagnostics()}));
   912	    }
   913	    if method == Method::Get && path == "/dev/clock/status" { return maintenance::clock_status(request, shared); }
   914	    if method == Method::Options {
   915	        respond_empty(request, 204);
   916	        return;
   917	    }
   918	    if method == Method::Post && path == "/seed" && !shared.personas {
   919	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "seeding is a development door")) }));
   920	    }
   921	    if path == "/dev/reset" && !shared.personas {
   922	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&dev_doors::reset_release()) }));
   923	    }
   924	    // Check identity before upload admission or allocating any large body.
   925	    // The route rechecks after reading, so expiry/revocation during a slow
   926	    // upload cannot retain the authority held at its start.
   927	    // Logout's body is only 4 KiB and its sole authentication happens inside
   928	    // the SQLite writer transaction that closes the live session family.
   929	    if method == Method::Post && (path == "/assets" || path.starts_with("/assets/intent/") || path == "/sync" || path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/following/") || path.starts_with("/m/") || path.starts_with("/a/")
   930	        || (path.starts_with("/actions/") && path.ends_with("/cancel"))
   931	        || matches!(path.as_str(), "/auth/refresh" | "/auth/logout-all")) {
   932	        if let Err(refusal) = principal_of(&request, shared) {
   933	            // No authenticated viewer: metadata cannot disclose a store head.
   934	            if path.starts_with("/q/") { return respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)})); }
   935	            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
   936	        }
   937	    }
   938	    if method == Method::Post && (path == "/auth/revoke" || path.starts_with("/routes/verify/") || (path.starts_with("/actions/") && !path.ends_with("/cancel"))) {
   939	        if let Err(refusal) = service_of(&request, shared) {
   940	            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
   941	        }
   942	    }
   943	    if method == Method::Get && matches!(path.as_str(), "/" | "/schema" | "/api.ts" | "/native.wasm" | "/changes") {
   944	        if let Err(refusal) = principal_of(&request, shared) {
   945	            return reject(request, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, refusal);
   946	        }
   947	    }
   948	    let _upload_permit = if method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" {
   949	        match crate::assets::admit(shared) {
   950	            Some(permit) => Some(permit),
   951	            None => {
   952	                let mut refusal = Refusal::new("E_RATE_LIMIT", "auth", "too many concurrent uploads; retry shortly");
   953	                refusal.retryable = true;
   954	                return respond_timed(request, shared, 429, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
   955	            }
   956	        }
   957	    } else { None };
   958	    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
   959	    // @ref LLP 3000.038#3 — a file upload streams at most the schema's largest file bound, refused before reading when declared larger.
   960	    let file_upload = method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" && crate::assets::is_file_upload(&url);
   961	    if method == Method::Post {
   962	        let ceiling = match path.as_str() {
   963	            "/assets" if file_upload => match crate::assets::file_ceiling(shared) {
   964	                Ok(ceiling) => ceiling,
   965	                Err(refusal) => return reject(request, 415, refusal),
   966	            },
   967	            "/assets" => crate::assets::UPLOAD_CEILING,
   968	            "/seed" | "/dev/reset" => SEED_REQUEST_CEILING,
   969	            "/sync" => SYNC_REQUEST_CEILING,
   970	            path if path.starts_with("/auth/") => crate::auth::BODY_CEILING,
   971	            path if path.starts_with("/following/") => 64 * 1024,
   972	            _ => JSON_REQUEST_CEILING,
   973	        };
   974	        let length = request.body_length();
   975	        let too_large = || if file_upload {
   976	            Refusal::new("E_ASSET_TOO_LARGE", "asset", format!("the file exceeds {ceiling} bytes, the largest file column's bound")).guide("assets")
   977	        } else {
   978	            Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("the request body exceeds its {ceiling}-byte limit; split it into smaller requests"))
   979	        };
   980	        if length.is_some_and(|length| length > ceiling) {
   981	            return reject(request, 413, too_large());
   982	        }
   983	        let mut buffer = [0; 8 * 1024];
   984	        loop {
   985	            let length = buffer.len().min(ceiling - bytes.len() + 1);
   986	            match request.as_reader().read(&mut buffer[..length]) {
   987	                Ok(0) => break,
   988	                Ok(read) if read > ceiling - bytes.len() => return reject(request, 413, too_large()),
   989	                Ok(read) => bytes.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..read]),
   990	                Err(error) if error.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
   991	                Err(error) => return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("cannot read the request body: {error}"))),
   992	            }
   993	        }
   994	        if length.is_some_and(|length| length != bytes.len()) {
   995	            return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "the request body ended before its declared Content-Length"));
   996	        }
   997	    }
   998	    if method == Method::Post && path == "/assets" {
   999	        return crate::assets::upload(request, bytes, shared);
  1000	    }
  1001	    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id)=path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s|s.strip_suffix("/declare")){return crate::assets::declare(request,id,bytes,shared);}
  1002	    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix("/finish")) {
  1003	        return crate::assets::finish(request, id, bytes, shared);
  1004	    }
  1005	    if method == Method::Post && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/intent/").and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix("/cancel")) {
  1006	        return crate::assets::cancel(request, id, shared);
  1007	    }
  1008	    if method == Method::Get && path == "/assets/intents" { return crate::assets::intents(request, shared); }
  1009	    if method == Method::Get && let Some(id) = path.strip_prefix("/assets/") {
  1010	        return crate::assets::serve(request, id, shared);
  1011	    }
  1012	    let body = match String::from_utf8(bytes) {
  1013	        Ok(body) => body,
  1014	        Err(_) => return reject(request, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "a JSON request body must be valid UTF-8")),
  1015	    };
  1016	    let request_now = if (method == Method::Get && path == "/changes")
  1017	        || (method == Method::Post && (path == "/sync" || path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/m/")))
  1018	    {
  1019	        match shared.advance_time_audiences().map_err(|error| error.refusal()) {
  1020	            Ok(now) => Some(now),
  1021	            Err(refusal) => return reject(request, 503, refusal),
  1022	        }
  1023	    } else { None };
  1024	    if operation && !shared.personas {crate::release_requests::Scope::admit(&shared.release_requests,||*lock(&shared.generation));}
  1025	    match (method, path.as_str()) {
  1026	        (Method::Post, "/dev/clock/advance") => maintenance::advance(request, shared, &body),
  1027	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/jobs/") => maintenance::run_job(request, shared, &path["/jobs/".len()..], &body),
  1028	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/dev/jobs/") => maintenance::run_job(request, shared, &path["/dev/jobs/".len()..], &body),
  1029	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/a/") => actions::call(request, shared, path.trim_start_matches("/a/"), &body),
  1030	        (Method::Post, "/actions/claim") => actions::claim(request, shared, &body),
  1031	        (Method::Post, "/actions/claim-set") => actions::claim_set(request, shared, &body),
  1032	        (Method::Post, "/routes/verify/claim") => routes::claim(request, shared, &body),
  1033	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/routes/verify/") => routes::settle(request, shared, path, &body),
  1034	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/actions/") => actions::settle(request, shared, path, &body),
  1035	        (Method::Post, "/effects/claim") => effects::claim(request, shared, &body),
  1036	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/effects/") => effects::post(request, shared, path, &body),
  1037	        (Method::Get, "/effects") => effects::list(request, shared, &url),
  1038	        (Method::Get | Method::Post, "/probe") => match principal_of(&request, shared) {
  1039	            Ok(viewer) => {
  1040	                let identity = shared.read_identity();
  1041	                match identity {
  1042	                    Ok((store_id, seq,generation)) => {
  1043	                        let mut reply = json!({ "ok": true, "store_id": store_id, "seq": seq, "generation":generation,"viewer": viewer.0 });
  1044	                        if query_params(&url).get("scope").is_some_and(|scope| scope == "1") {
  1045	                            reply["kind"] = json!(viewer.1); reply["scope"] = json!(viewer.2);
  1046	                        }
  1047	                        respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply)
  1048	                    },
  1049	                    Err(error) => respond_timed(request, shared, 500, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&error) })),
  1050	                }
  1051	            },
  1052	            Err(refusal) => respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else { 401 }, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) })),
  1053	        },
  1054	        (Method::Get, "/auth/config") => {
  1055	            let (auth, environment) = {
  1056	                let engine = producing_engine(shared);
  1057	                let environment = if shared.personas { "development" }
  1058	                    else if engine.store().meta("deployment_environment").ok().flatten().as_deref() == Some("staging") { "staging" } else { "production" };
  1059	                (engine.schema().auth.clone(), environment)
  1060	            };
     1	//! Immutable source releases with a database-authoritative activation pointer.
     2	use super::*;
     3	use crate::store_owner::Owner;
     4	use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
     5	#[path = "staging.rs"]
     6	mod staging;
     7	struct Stage(PathBuf);
     8	impl Drop for Stage {fn drop(&mut self){let _=std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);}}
     9	
    10	fn directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    11	    options
    12	        .positionals
    13	        .first()
    14	        .map(PathBuf::from)
    15	        .ok_or_else(|| "a deployment directory is required".into())
    16	}
    17	
    18	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000#5.1 — deployment aliases share ownership, control and worker paths.
    19	fn canonical_directory(options: &Options) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    20	    let requested = directory(options)?;
    21	    if !requested.exists() {
    22	        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&requested).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    23	    }
    24	    let dir = requested.canonicalize().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    25	    snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&dir).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    26	    Ok(dir)
    27	}
    28	
    29	/// `dev` and `serve` hold the store-owner lock for their lifetime. Release
    30	/// commands route to the admitted server when it owns this deployment.
    31	pub(crate) fn deployment_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<Owner, String> {
    32	    crate::store_owner::command(&dir.join("store.sqlite"))
    33	}
    34	fn current(dir: &Path, owner: &Owner) -> Result<Option<u64>, String> {
    35	    let database = dir.join("store.sqlite");
    36	    if database.exists() {
    37	        let store = owner
    38	            .open_store(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))
    39	            .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    40	        if let Some(release) = store.meta("release").map_err(|e| e.to_string())? {
    41	            return release
    42	                .parse()
    43	                .map(Some)
    44	                .map_err(|_| "invalid release identity in store".into());
    45	        }
    46	    }
    47	    // Compatibility with deployments made before the database owned this
    48	    // pointer. The next activation records it transactionally.
    49	    match std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("current")) {
    50	        Ok(text) => text
    51	            .trim()
    52	            .parse()
    53	            .map(Some)
    54	            .map_err(|_| "invalid current release file".into()),
    55	        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => Ok(None),
    56	        Err(e) => Err(format!("cannot read current release: {e}")),
    57	    }
    58	}
    59	pub(super) fn bound_at(root: PathBuf) -> Result<snapback4_lang::Bound, String> {
    60	    let project = Project { root };
    61	    if project.sources().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.is_empty() {
    62	        return Err("release has no Quarry source; select a complete immutable release".into());
    63	    }
    64	    let bound = project.compile().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    65	    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
    66	        return Err(bound
    67	            .diagnostics
    68	            .iter()
    69	            .map(ToString::to_string)
    70	            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
    71	            .join("\n"));
    72	    }
    73	    Ok(bound)
    74	}
    75	fn check_change(
    76	    dir: &Path,
    77	    previous: Option<u64>,
    78	    bound: &snapback4_lang::Bound,
    79	    options: &Options,
    80	    target_release: Option<u64>,
    81	) -> Result<(), String> {
    82	    if !options.flags.contains_key("check") && let Some(n) = previous {
    83	        let old = bound_at(dir.join("releases").join(n.to_string()))?;
    84	        let store = snapback4_core::Store::open_read_only(&dir.join("store.sqlite")).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    85	        let target_label = target_release.map(|n| format!("release {n}")).unwrap_or_else(|| "the new release".into());
    86	        let lines = super::release_narration::compatibility(&store, &old.schema, &bound.schema, n, &target_label, target_release.is_none())?;
    87	        for line in lines {
    88	            eprintln!("{line}");
    89	        }
    90	    }
    91	    if options.flags.contains_key("allow-destructive") {
    92	        eprintln!(
    93	            "deploy/rollback retain storage; --allow-destructive does not bypass validation or prune. To drop dormant storage: snapback4 prune {} --allow-destructive",
    94	            dir.display()
    95	        );
    96	    }
    97	    Ok(())
    98	}
    99	
   100	fn command_lock(dir: &Path) -> Result<std::fs::File, String> {
   101	    #[cfg(unix)]
   102	    {
   103	        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(
   104	            &dir.join("store.sqlite"),
   105	            &dir.join(".release-command"),
   106	            true,
   107	            0,
   108	        )
   109	        .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   110	        file.lock().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   220	    }
   221	}
   222	
   223	pub fn deploy(options: &Options) -> i32 {
   224	    report(
   225	        "deploy",
   226	        (|| {
   227	            let dir = canonical_directory(options)?;
   228	            let _command = command_lock(&dir)?;
   229	            let existed = dir.join("store.sqlite").exists();
   230	            let checking=options.flags.contains_key("check");
   231	            let _deployment = if checking {None} else {target(&dir)?};
   232	            if let Some(owner) = &_deployment { owner.report_reclaim(options.flags.contains_key("verbose")); }
   233	            let project = super::proof_source::project(options, "schema")?;
   234	            let sources = super::proof_source::sources(&project, options, "schema")?;
   235	            let bound = compiled_or_exit(super::proof_source::compile(&sources, options, "schema"), options.json);

     3	/// Who is asking: a bearer session, or (in `dev`, from loopback) a persona.
     4	pub(crate) fn principal_of(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<snapback4_core::identity::Capability, Refusal> {
     5	    principal_with_owner(request, shared, None)
     6	}
     7	
     8	fn principal_with_owner(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, owner: Option<&Engine>) -> Result<snapback4_core::identity::Capability, Refusal> {
     9	    // Declared public bearer routes keep their existing development admission.
    10	    if !(request.method() == &Method::Post && routes::declared(shared, request.url()).is_some()
    11	        && !request.headers().iter().any(|h| h.field.equiv("x-snapback-persona"))) {
    12	        dev_request(request, shared)?;
    13	    }
    14	    let mut persona = None;
    15	    let mut bearer = None;
    16	    for header in request.headers() {
    17	        let name = header.field.as_str().as_str().to_ascii_lowercase();
    18	        let value = header.value.as_str().trim();
    19	        if name == "x-snapback-persona" {
    20	            if persona.is_some()
    21	                || value.is_empty()
    22	                || !value.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_' || c == '-')
    23	            {
    24	                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send exactly one valid x-snapback-persona header").guide("personas"));
    25	            }
    26	            persona = Some(value.to_owned());
    27	        }
    28	        if name == "authorization" {
    29	            let Some(token) = crate::auth::parse_bearer(value) else {
    30	                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send Authorization: Bearer <token>").guide("personas"));
    31	            };
    32	            if bearer.replace(token.to_owned()).is_some() {
    33	                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send exactly one Authorization header").guide("personas"));
    34	            }
    35	        }
    36	    }
    37	    if persona.is_some() && bearer.is_some() {
    38	        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "send one credential: a development persona or a bearer token").guide("personas"));
    39	    }
    40	    if let Some(persona) = persona {
    41	        if let Some(engine) = owner { shared.dev_authority(engine)?; }
    42	        if shared.dev_doors() {
    43	            // `use personas` is the cast: a test expecting an outsider to be
    44	            // refused must not see it signed in. No declaration admits none.
    45	            // (Taken briefly; the engine
    46	            // guard, when held, is always acquired before this one.)
    47	            let declared = lock(&shared.bound).personas.clone();
    48	            if !declared.contains(&persona) {
    49	                return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", format!(
    50	                    "persona {persona} is not declared; `use personas {}` is this project's cast. Add {persona} to it (dev re-adopts on save) or send a declared persona", declared.join(", ")))
    51	                    .rewrite(format!("use personas {}{persona}", if declared.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("{}, ", declared.join(", ")) })).guide("personas"));
    52	            }
    53	            return Ok((persona_principal(&persona), "persona".into(), snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full));
    54	        }
    55	        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "personas are a development door; a deployed server takes bearer tokens (snapback4/auth)").guide("personas"));
    56	    }
    57	    if let Some(token) = bearer {
    58	        // Reads may authenticate against the committed generation during adoption.
    59	        // Keep write authentication behind the writer: time queued for adoption
    60	        // must not preserve a session that expired or was revoked in that wait.
    61	        let path = request.url().split('?').next().unwrap_or("");
    62	        let read = detached_request(request.method(), path);
    63	        let resolve = |engine: &Engine, identity: Option<&str>, detached: bool| {
    64	            let now = shared.now();
    65	            let sample = snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample { now, supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(Ordering::Acquire) };
    66	            let result = if detached {
    67	                snapback4_core::identity::resolve_read_only_supervised(engine.store(), &token, sample, identity)
    68	            } else {
    69	                snapback4_core::identity::resolve_supervised(engine.store(), &token, sample)
    70	            };
    71	            let result=result.map_err(|error| error.refusal()).and_then(|value|value.ok_or_else(||
    72	                snapback4_core::identity::diagnostics::refused(engine.store(), &token, now, trusted_dev_request(request, shared))));
    73	            if result.is_err() {crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());}
    74	            result
    75	        };
    76	        return if let Some(engine) = owner { resolve(engine, None, false) }
    77	        else if read { match shared.reader() {
    78	            Ok((epoch, reader)) => {
    79	                let identity = lock(&shared.wake).store_id.clone();
    80	                let result = resolve(&reader, identity.as_deref(), true);
    81	                lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
    82	                result
    83	            }
    84	            Err(_) if shared.memory_store() => resolve(&lock(&shared.engine), None, false),
    85	            Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
    86	        } } else { resolve(&lock(&shared.engine), None, false) };
    87	    }
    88	    Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "sign in: send a bearer token (snapback4/auth), or x-snapback-persona in development").guide("personas"))
    89	}
    90	
    91	// @ref LLP 3000.000.000.001#4-development-doors — a persona cannot cross from authentication into a replacement password-only reader.
    92	pub(crate) fn persona_read_authority(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    93	    let Some(persona) = request.headers().iter().find(|header| header.field.equiv("x-snapback-persona")) else { return Ok(()) };
    94	    shared.dev_authority(engine)?;
    95	    let bound = lock(&shared.bound);
    96	    if (!shared.unsafe_dev_doors && bound.schema.auth.password && !bound.schema.auth.guests)
    97	        || !bound.personas.iter().any(|name| name == persona.value.as_str().trim()) {
    98	        return Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "persona authority changed while this read waited; sign in with a password or use the current declared development cast").guide("staging"));
    99	    }
   100	    // The cast is checked at this read's admission. Never hold its publication
   101	    // mutex during I/O: concurrent ordinary writes still need to authenticate.
   102	    // The engine transaction fences a replaced execution schema.
   103	    Ok(())
   104	}

   106	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — adoption's writer queue cannot retain expired bearer authority.
   107	pub(crate) fn mutation_authority(request: &Request, shared: &Shared, engine: &Engine, principal: &str) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
   108	    if principal_with_owner(request, shared, Some(engine))?.0 == principal { Ok(()) }
   109	    else { Err(Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "the request's session changed; sign in again").guide("personas")) }
   110	}

   112	// @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — additions require an explicit decision here.
   113	pub(super) fn read_permitted(method: &Method, path: &str) -> bool {
   114	    match method {
   115	        Method::Get => matches!(path, "/changes" | "/probe" | "/schema") || path.starts_with("/assets/"),
   116	        Method::Post => matches!(path, "/sync" | "/probe" | "/auth/refresh" | "/auth/logout") || path.starts_with("/q/"),
   117	        _ => false,
   118	    }
   119	}

     3	const SOURCE: &str = "use identity\ntable items:\n  n: int\n  public 'fixture'\n  update <- allow\njob mark():\n  for item in items:\n    update items[item] { n: 9 }\n";
     4	
     5	fn close_doors(shared: &Shared, engine: &mut Engine) {
     6	    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), SOURCE.replace("use identity", "use identity password"))]);
     7	    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:?}", bound.diagnostics);
     8	    let next = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
     9	    lock(&shared.readers).replace(engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
    10	    *lock(&shared.bound) = bound;
    11	    *lock(&shared.generation) = next;
    12	}

    15	fn stage_slow_dev_bodies_cannot_retain_authority_after_password_adoption() {
    16	    for (path, payload) in [("/seed", "[]"), ("/dev/reset", "{}"), ("/dev/clock/advance", r#"{"duration":"+1h"}"#), ("/dev/jobs/mark", "{}")] {
    17	        let host = Host::with_default_doors(SOURCE);
    18	        let before = host.shared.now();
    19	        let mut stream = TcpStream::connect(host.address).unwrap();
    20	        stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(5))).unwrap();
    21	        write!(stream, "POST {path} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nExpect: 100-continue\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\nConnection: close\r\n\r\n", payload.len()).unwrap();
    22	        let mut interim = [0; 128];
    23	        let n = stream.read(&mut interim).unwrap();
    24	        assert!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&interim[..n]).contains("100 Continue"), "passed the pre-body door fence");
    25	        close_doors(&host.shared, &mut lock(&host.shared.engine));
    26	        stream.write_all(payload.as_bytes()).unwrap();
    27	        let mut response = String::new(); stream.read_to_string(&mut response).unwrap();
    28	        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH", "{path}: {response}");
    29	        assert!(host.shared.now() - before < 5000, "refused clock did not advance");
    30	    }

    49	fn stage_persona_cannot_acquire_a_replacement_password_only_reader() {
    50	    for file in [false, true] {
    51	        let dir = ReaderTestDirectory::new();
    52	        let store = if file { snapback4_core::Store::open(&dir.0.join("store.sqlite")).unwrap() }
    53	            else { snapback4_core::Store::memory().unwrap() };
    54	        let source = "use identity\nuse personas alice\nquery secret():\n  return 1\n";
    55	        let host = Host::with_default_doors_store(source, store);
    56	        let (entered, waiting) = mpsc::channel();
    57	        let barrier = Arc::new((Mutex::new(false), Condvar::new()));
    58	        let held = barrier.clone();
    59	        let once = std::sync::atomic::AtomicBool::new(false);
    60	        *lock(&host.shared.reader_open) = Some(Arc::new(move || {
    61	            if once.swap(true, Ordering::AcqRel) { return; }
    62	            entered.send(()).unwrap();
    63	            let (state, wake) = &*held;
    64	            let mut ready = lock(state);
    65	            while !*ready { ready = wake.wait(ready).unwrap(); }
    66	        }));
    67	        let address = host.address;
    68	        let task = thread::spawn(move || exchange_held(address, Duration::from_secs(10),
    69	            "POST /q/secret HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nx-snapback-persona: alice\r\nContent-Length: 11\r\n\r\n{\"args\":{}}"));
    70	        waiting.recv_timeout(Duration::from_secs(5)).unwrap();
    71	        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.replace("use identity", "use identity password").replace("return 1", "return 9876"))]);
    72	        {
    73	            let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
    74	            let generation = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p| p.program.clone())).unwrap();
    75	            lock(&host.shared.readers).replace(&engine, &bound.refusal_sites);
    76	            *lock(&host.shared.bound) = bound;
    77	            *lock(&host.shared.generation) = generation;
    78	        }
    79	        *lock(&barrier.0) = true; barrier.1.notify_all();
    80	        let response = task.join().unwrap();
    81	        assert_eq!(status(&response), 401, "file={file}: {response}");
    82	        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH");
    83	        assert!(body(&response).get("data").is_none(), "new result never crosses the closed persona door");
    84	    }
    85	}

   116	            assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"], "E_AUTH");
   117	            let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
   118	            let after: i64 = engine.store().conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _requests", [], |row|row.get(0)).unwrap();
   119	            assert_eq!(after, before, "refused admission creates no durable work");
   120	            assert!(!engine.action_request_at("keep", "dev:alice", host.shared.now()).unwrap().cancelled);
   121	            let claims = engine.claim_actions("service:worker", None, 10, 1000, 60, host.shared.now()).unwrap();
   122	            assert_eq!(claims.len(), 1, "only previously admitted work is claimable");
   123	            assert_eq!(claims[0].request.id, "keep");
   124	        }
   125	    }
   126	}

   129	fn stage_asset_owner_transitions_recheck_at_the_writer() {
   130	    let source = "use identity\nuse personas alice\n";
   131	    for path in ["/assets/intent/abc/cancel", "/assets/intent/abc/declare", "/assets/intent/abc/finish", "/assets/intents"] {
   132	        let host = Host::with_default_doors(source);
   133	        let mut engine = lock(&host.shared.engine);
   134	        let store = engine.store().meta("store_id").unwrap().unwrap();
   135	        let address = host.address;
   136	        let task = thread::spawn(move || {
   137	            let payload = if path.ends_with("declare") { json!({"digest":"a".repeat(64),"placement":{"op":"place","args":{},"argument":"photo"}}) }
   138	                else if path.ends_with("finish") { json!({"op":"place","args":{},"why":{"code":"REFUSED","family":"rule","message":"fixture"}}) }
   139	                else { json!({}) }.to_string();
   140	            let method = if path == "/assets/intents" { "GET" } else { "POST" };
   141	            exchange_held(address, Duration::from_secs(10), &format!("{method} {path}?store_id={store} HTTP/1.1\r\nHost: localhost\r\nConnection: close\r\nx-snapback-persona: alice\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{payload}",payload.len()))
   142	        });
   143	        wait_until(|| host.shared.engine.waiting() > 0);
   144	        let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),source.replace("use identity", "use identity password"))]);
   145	        let generation = engine.adopt(bound.schema.clone(), bound.programs.iter().map(|p|p.program.clone())).unwrap();
   146	        lock(&host.shared.readers).replace(&engine,&bound.refusal_sites);
   147	        *lock(&host.shared.bound)=bound;*lock(&host.shared.generation)=generation;
   148	        drop(engine);
   149	        let response=task.join().unwrap();
   150	        assert_eq!(body(&response)["denied"]["code"],"E_AUTH","{path}: {response}");
   151	        let engine=lock(&host.shared.engine);
   152	        let intents:i64=engine.store().conn().query_row("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM _asset_intents",[],|row|row.get(0)).unwrap();
   153	        assert_eq!(intents,0,"refused owner request cannot leave an intent or cancellation tombstone");
   154	    }
   155	}

    36	test("staging accounts are real password users; service grants obey rules; marker survives release switches", { timeout: 90000 }, async t => {
    37	  const cwd = fixture(t);
    38	  await deploy("staging", "snapback", { cwd, args: ["--staging"] });
    39	  let host = await startServe("staging", { cwd, seedUsers });
    40	  try {
    41	    assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging");
    42	    assert.equal((await config(host)).development, false);
    43	    for (const [role, accounts] of Object.entries(host.sessions)) {
    44	      assert.equal(accounts.length, 2);
    45	      for (const account of accounts) {
    46	        assert.ok(account.principal.startsWith("user:"));
    47	        const signed = await login(host.url, account.email, account.password); assert.equal(signed.ok, true);
    48	        const me = await post(host, "/q/myRole", signed.session.token); assert.equal(me.data.role, role);
    49	        assert.equal((await post(host, "/m/add", signed.session.token, { body: "real user data" })).state, "sent");
    50	        const forged = await post(host, "/m/provision", signed.session.token, { principal: account.principal, role: "admin" });
    51	        assert.equal(forged.why.code, "NOT_OPERATOR");
    52	      }
    53	    }
    54	    for (const path of ["/seed", "/dev/reset", "/dev/clock/advance", "/dev/jobs/foo", "/dev/token"]) {
    55	      assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + path, { method: "POST", body: "[]" })).status, 403, path);
    56	    }
    57	    assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + "/q/myRole", { method: "POST", headers: { "x-snapback-persona": "alice" }, body: '{"args":{}}' })).status, 401);
    58	    const v2 = join(cwd, "v2"); mkdirSync(v2); writeFileSync(join(v2, "schema.q"), schema + "\nquery version():\n  return 2\n");
    59	    await deploy("staging", v2, { cwd }); assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging");
    60	    await rollback("staging", 1, { cwd }); assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging");
    61	    const repeated = await cli(["deploy", "staging", "--schema", "snapback", "--staging"], { cwd });
    62	    assert.equal(repeated.code, 0, repeated.stderr);
    63	    const token = await host.operatorToken();
    64	    for (const file of readdirSync(join(cwd, "logs"))) assert.ok(!readFileSync(join(cwd, "logs", file), "utf8").includes(token), "operator token never enters logs");
    65	  } finally { await host.stop(); }
    66	  host = await startServe("staging", { cwd });
    67	  try { assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "staging"); } finally { await host.stop(); }
    68	});

    70	test("production cannot be relabelled; bulk setup refuses before token mint or account creation; first admin uses explicit operator", { timeout: 60000 }, async t => {
    71	  const cwd = fixture(t);
    72	  await deploy("production", "snapback", { cwd });
    73	  let host = await startServe("production", { cwd });
    74	  try {
    75	    assert.equal((await config(host)).environment, "production");
    76	    let minted = false;
    77	    await assert.rejects(seedAccounts({ url: host.url, operatorToken: async () => { minted = true; return "must not mint"; } }, seedUsers), /E_STAGING:/);
    78	    assert.equal(minted, false);
    79	    const relabel = await cli(["deploy", "production", "--staging"], { cwd });
    80	    assert.notEqual(relabel.code, 0); assert.match(relabel.stderr, /E_STAGING.*cannot be relabelled/);
    81	    const account = await signup(host.url, "first@example.test", "operator-selected-password"); assert.equal(account.ok, true);
    82	    assert.equal((await post(host, "/q/myRole", account.session.token)).data, null, "signup grants no role");
    83	    assert.equal((await post(host, "/m/provision", account.session.token, { principal: account.session.principal, role: "admin" })).why.code, "NOT_OPERATOR");
    84	    assert.equal((await post(host, "/m/provision", await host.operatorToken(), { principal: account.session.principal, role: "admin" })).state, "sent");
    85	    assert.equal((await post(host, "/q/myRole", account.session.token)).data.role, "admin");
    86	  } finally { await host.stop(); }
    87	  await assert.rejects(startServe("production", { cwd, seedUsers }), /E_STAGING:/, "failed setup cleans up its own host");
    88	  const relabel = await cli(["deploy", "production", "--staging"], { cwd }); assert.notEqual(relabel.code, 0);
    89	});

    91	test("password dev closes fixture and persona doors; explicit opt-in, declared cast, and persistent defaults", { timeout: 90000 }, async t => {
    92	  const cwd = fixture(t);
    93	  let host = await startDev({ cwd, args: ["--no-watch"] });

    95	    assert.deepEqual(await config(host).then(({ development, devDoors, environment }) => ({ development, devDoors, environment })), { development: true, devDoors: false, environment: "development" });

    98	      assert.equal(response.status, 403, path);
    99	    }
   100	    await assert.rejects(host.now(), /unsafe-dev-doors/);
   101	    const account = await signup(host.url, "persistent@example.test", "persistent-password"); assert.equal(account.ok, true);
   102	  } finally { await host.stop(); }
   103	  host = await startDev({ cwd, args: ["--unsafe-dev-doors", "--no-watch"] });
   104	  try {
   105	    assert.equal((await config(host)).devDoors, true);
   106	    assert.equal((await login(host.url, "persistent@example.test", "persistent-password")).ok, true, "default dev preserves password verifiers across restart");
   107	    assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + "/dev/status")).status, 200);
   108	    assert.equal((await fetch(host.url + "/q/myRole", { method: "POST", headers: { "x-snapback-persona": "undeclared" }, body: '{"args":{}}' })).status, 401, "empty cast admits no persona");
   109	  } finally { await host.stop(); }
   110	  host = await startDev({ cwd, args: ["--memory", "--no-watch"] });
   111	  try { assert.equal((await login(host.url, "persistent@example.test", "persistent-password")).ok, false, "memory explicitly isolates accounts"); }
   112	  finally { await host.stop(); }
   113	});

     1	//! Authenticated, bounded controller transport. Custody is per consumer, never
     2	//! an operation-global cursor shared by tabs. Bodies contain no work counters.
     3	use super::*;
     4	use snapback4_core::engine::following_history::{Request as UnitRequest, projection};
     5	
     6	pub(super) fn serve(request: Request, shared: &Shared, operation: &str, body: &str) {
     7	    if body.len() > 64 * 1024 {
     8	        return respond_timed(
     9	            request,
    10	            shared,
    11	            400,
    12	            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
    13	        );
    14	    }
    15	    let (principal, _, scope) = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
    16	        Ok(p) => p,
    17	        Err(r) => return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&r)})),
    18	    };
    19	    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — Following custody is outside the closed read allow-list.
    20	    // The outer guard refuses before body intake; retain the boundary here too.
    21	    if scope == snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Read {
    22	        let why = Refusal::new(
    23	            "E_READ_ONLY",
    24	            "auth",
    25	            "Following history requires a full session; ordinary inbox queries remain available",
    26	        )
    27	        .guide("read-only");
    28	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
    29	    }
    30	    let payload: Json = match serde_json::from_str(body) {
    31	        Ok(v) => v,
    32	        Err(_) => {
    33	            return respond_timed(
    34	                request,
    35	                shared,
    36	                400,
    37	                json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
    38	            );
    39	        }
    40	    };
    41	    // Lock order is engine then controller. Authentication is checked again at
    42	    // the same engine incarnation that owns the source read transaction.
    43	    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    44	    crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
    45	    if let Err(r) = mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal) {
    46	        drop(engine);
    47	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&r)}));
    48	    }
    49	    let Some(program) = engine.program(operation) else {
    50	        drop(engine);
    51	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"phase":"restart"}));
    52	    };
    53	    let Some(plan) = projection::plan(engine.schema(), program) else {
    54	        drop(engine);
    55	        return respond_timed(
    56	            request,
    57	            shared,
    58	            200,
    59	            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_DECLARATION"}),
    60	        );
    61	    };
    62	    let binding = plan.binding;
    63	    let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
    64	    let Some(fields) = payload.get("args").and_then(Json::as_object) else {
    65	        drop(engine);
    66	        return respond_timed(
    67	            request,
    68	            shared,
    69	            400,
    70	            json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
    71	        );
    72	    };
    73	    if let Some(why)=super::dev_doors::persona_argument(shared,&program.args,&Json::Object(fields.clone())) {
    74	        drop(engine);return respond_timed(request,shared,400,json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT","message":why.message,"denied":why}));
    75	    }
    76	    for (name, value) in fields {
    77	        let Some(value) = program
    78	            .args
    79	            .get(name)
    80	            .and_then(|kind| wire::from_json(kind, value).ok())
    81	        else {
    82	            drop(engine);
    83	            return respond_timed(
    84	                request,
    85	                shared,
    86	                400,
    87	                json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}),
    88	            );
    89	        };
    90	        args.insert(name.clone(), value);
    91	    }
    92	    let now = shared.now();
    93	    let mut reader = lock(&shared.following);
    94	    let action = payload
    95	        .get("action")
    96	        .and_then(Json::as_str)
    97	        .unwrap_or("unit");
    98	    let answer = if action == "open" {
    99	        reader
   100	            .opening_projected(&engine, operation, Context::new(&principal, args).at(now))
   101	            .map(|o| json!({"opening":o.request.opening}).to_string())
   102	    } else if let Some(opening) = payload.get("opening").and_then(Json::as_str) {
   103	        if action == "cancel" {
   104	            reader
   105	                .cancel_projected(
   106	                    &mut engine,
   107	                    opening,
   108	                    operation,
   109	                    Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
   110	                )
   111	                .map(|_| json!({"phase":"restart"}).to_string())
   112	        } else if action == "refresh" {
   113	            let mounted = serde_json::from_value::<
   114	                Vec<snapback4_core::engine::following_history::Mounted>,
   115	            >(payload.get("mounted").cloned().unwrap_or(json!([])));
   116	            let seen = serde_json::from_value::<Vec<String>>(
   117	                payload.get("seen").cloned().unwrap_or(json!([])),
   118	            );
   119	            match (mounted, seen) {
   120	                (Ok(mounted), Ok(seen)) => reader
   121	                    .refresh_slice_metered(
   122	                        &mut engine,
   123	                        opening,
   124	                        operation,
   125	                        Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
   126	                        &mounted,
   127	                        &seen,
   128	                    )
   129	                    .map(|(r, _)| serde_json::to_string(&r).unwrap()),
   130	                _ => Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string()),
   131	            }
   132	        } else if action == "unit"
   133	            && payload
   134	                .get("token")
   135	                .is_some_and(|v| !v.is_null() && !v.is_string())
   136	        {
   137	            Ok(json!({"phase":"restart"}).to_string())
   138	        } else if action == "unit" {
   139	            let q = UnitRequest {
   140	                declaration: binding.declaration,
   141	                page_size: binding.page_size,
   142	                opening: opening.into(),
   143	                token: payload
   144	                    .get("token")
   145	                    .and_then(Json::as_str)
   146	                    .map(str::to_owned),
   147	            };
   148	            reader
   149	                .request_projected(
   150	                    &mut engine,
   151	                    q,
   152	                    Context::new(&principal, args).at(now),
   153	                    operation,
   154	                )
   155	                .map(|out| out.answer.bytes().to_owned())
   156	        } else {
   157	            Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string())
   158	        }
   159	    } else {
   160	        Ok(json!({"phase":"refused","code":"E_FOLLOWING_INPUT"}).to_string())
   161	    };
   162	    drop(reader);
   163	    drop(engine);
   164	    let bytes =
   165	        answer.unwrap_or_else(|e| json!({"phase":"refused","code":e.refusal().code}).to_string());
   166	    // Do not parse/re-encode a pinned response or attach a fresh store counter.
   167	    let mut response = Response::from_string(bytes);
   168	    if let Some(header)=crate::serve::generation_header(){response.add_header(header);}
   169	    response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Content-Type", "application/json").unwrap());
   170	    response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Cache-Control", "private, no-store").unwrap());
   171	    for header in cors(&request).into_iter().chain(latency::header()) {
   172	        response.add_header(header);
   173	    }
   174	    let _ = request.respond(response);
   175	}
  1060	            };
  1061	            // @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#1 — an opt-in proof marker asserts the directly bound server, without granting authority.
  1062	            let mut config = json!({ "guests": auth.guests, "password": auth.password, "development": shared.personas, "devDoors": shared.dev_doors(), "environment": environment });
  1063	            if let Ok(id) = std::env::var("SNAPBACK4_PROOF_ID") && !id.is_empty() {
  1064	                config["proof_id"] = json!(id);
  1065	            }
  1066	            respond_timed(request, shared, 200, config);
  1067	        }
  1068	        (Method::Get, "/") => {
  1069	            let seq = match shared.read_head() {
  1070	                Ok((_, seq)) => seq,
  1071	                Err(error) => return respond_timed(request, shared, 500, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&error) })),
  1072	            };

  1076	                let tables: Vec<Json> = bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(name, table)| json!({ "name": name, "sync": table.sync.as_ref().map(|s| s.line_in(table)), "fields": table.columns.iter().filter(|(column, _)| !table.private_field(column) && !table.engine_field(column)).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>() })).collect();

  1099	        }
  1100	        (Method::Get, "/native.wasm") => {

  1103	            let installed = {

  1105	                let installed=lock(&shared.native).clone();
  1106	                crate::release_requests::stamp(*lock(&shared.generation));
  1107	                installed
  1108	            };

  1111	                    let mut response = Response::from_data(module.bytes);

  1116	                    for header in cors(&request).into_iter().chain(latency::header()) { response.add_header(header); }
  1117	                    let _ = request.respond(response);
  1118	                }
  1119	                None => respond_timed(request, shared, 404, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&Refusal::new("E_NATIVE", "schema", "this backend has no native module")) })),
  1120	            }
  1121	        }
  1122	        (Method::Get, "/api.ts") => {
  1123	            let text={let bound=lock(&shared.bound);crate::release_requests::stamp(*lock(&shared.generation));snapback4_lang::emit::typescript(&bound)};
  1124	            let mut response = Response::from_string(text);
  1125	            if let Some(header)=generation_header(){response.add_header(header);}
  1126	            response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Content-Type", "text/plain; charset=utf-8").unwrap());
  1127	            response.add_header(Header::from_bytes("Cache-Control", "no-store").unwrap());

  1129	            let _ = request.respond(response);
  1130	        }
  1131	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/following/") => following::serve(request,shared,&path[11..],&body),
  1132	        (Method::Post, path) if path.starts_with("/q/") || path.starts_with("/m/") => {

  1137	            let (principal, _kind, scope) = match principal_of(&request, shared) {

  1144	            let manifest = if scope == snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Read { snapback4_core::engine::DeliveryManifest::Skip } else { snapback4_core::engine::DeliveryManifest::Build };

  1147	                Err(error) => {

  1152	            let delivery_fingerprint = if path.starts_with("/m/") {
  1153	                match ephemeral::delivery_fingerprint(&payload) {
  1154	                    Ok(value) => value,
  1155	                    Err(why) => return operation_refusal(request, shared, 200, why),
  1156	                }
  1157	            } else { None };
  1158	            let args_json = payload.get("args").cloned().unwrap_or(json!({}));
  1159	            let signature = if path.starts_with("/m/") {
  1160	                let id = payload.get("id").and_then(Json::as_str);
  1161	                #[cfg(test)] test_work(&shared.mutation_wait);
  1162	                let (replay, metadata, signature) = {
  1163	                    let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
  1164	                    if let Err(refusal) = mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal) {
  1165	                        drop(engine);
  1166	                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&refusal)}));
  1167	                    }
  1168	                    let replay = intent_store(&engine, &payload).and_then(|()| {
  1169	                        let replay = id.map(|id| engine.replay_mutation(&principal, id, &op, &args_json)).transpose().map_err(|e| e.refusal())?.flatten();
  1170	                        if replay.is_none() { engine.schema().check_delivery(&op, delivery_fingerprint.as_deref()).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?; }
  1171	                        Ok(replay)
  1172	                    });
  1173	                    if matches!(replay, Ok(None)) && engine.schema().operations.get(&op).is_some_and(|f| f.ephemeral) {
  1174	                        drop(engine);
  1175	                        return ephemeral::serve(request, shared, &op, &body);
  1176	                    }
  1177	                    // @ref LLP 3000.022#5 — decoding uses the signature whose binding
  1178	                    // passed preflight; adoption is rechecked by the executing writer.
  1179	                    let signature = engine.program(&op).map(|p| (p.kind, p.args.clone()));
  1180	                    (replay, response_head(&mut engine), signature)
  1181	                };
  1182	                let (store_id, head) = match metadata { Ok(head) => head, Err(why) => { respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({ "denied": why })); return; } };
  1183	                match replay {
  1184	                    Ok(Some(outcome)) => { let mut reply = outcome.reply(id); reply["store_id"] = json!(store_id); reply["head"] = json!(head); respond_timed(request, shared, 200, reply); return; }
  1185	                    Err(why) => { respond_timed(request, shared, if why.retryable { 503 } else { 200 }, mutation_reply(json!({ "state": "failed", "id": id, "why": why, "store_id": store_id, "head": head }))); return; }
  1186	                    Ok(None) => {}
  1187	                }
  1188	                #[cfg(test)] test_work(&shared.mutation_wait);
  1189	                signature
  1190	            } else {
  1191	                lock(&shared.bound).programs.iter().find(|p| p.program.name == op)
  1192	                    .map(|p| (p.program.kind, p.program.args.clone()))
  1193	            };
  1194	            // @ref LLP 3000.018#3.1 — refresh follows what a query may read on any path, never what one run read,
  1195	            // and comes from the schema of the engine that evaluates it.
  1196	            let presence_of = |engine: &Engine| engine.schema().operations.get(&op).is_some_and(|fact| fact.sites.iter().any(|site| site.kind == "presence"));
  1197	            let Some((kind, arg_types)) = signature else {
  1198	                if path.starts_with("/q/") && lock(&shared.readers).pending(&op) {
  1199	                    match shared.read_head() {
  1200	                        Ok((store_id, head)) => respond_timed(request, shared, 200, json!({"loading":true,"complete":false,"data":null,"store_id":store_id,"seq":head,"tables":[],"viewer":principal})),
  1201	                        Err(why) => respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied":why})),
  1202	                    }
  1203	                    return;
  1204	                }
  1205	                operation_refusal(request, shared, 404, dev_doors::stale(shared, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("unknown operation {op}")).guide("programs")));
  1206	                return;
  1207	            };
  1208	            if let Some(why) = dev_doors::persona_argument(shared, &arg_types, &args_json) { argument_refusal(request, shared, payload.get("id").and_then(Json::as_str), why); return; }
  1209	            let mut args = BTreeMap::new();
  1210	            if let Json::Object(fields) = &args_json {
  1211	                for (name, value) in fields {
  1212	                    match arg_types.get(name) {
  1213	                        Some(kind) => match wire::from_json(kind, value) {
  1214	                            Ok(typed) => {
  1215	                                args.insert(name.clone(), typed);
  1216	                            }
  1217	                            Err(why) => {
  1218	                                argument_refusal(request, shared, payload.get("id").and_then(Json::as_str), Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("argument {name}: {why}")).guide("types"));
  1219	                                return;
  1220	                            }
  1221	                        },
  1222	                        None => {
  1223	                            argument_refusal(request, shared, payload.get("id").and_then(Json::as_str), dev_doors::stale(shared, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", format!("{op} takes no argument {name}")).guide("programs")));
  1224	                            return;
  1225	                        }
  1226	                    }
  1227	                }
  1228	            }
  1229	            let viewer = principal.clone();
  1230	            let mut context = Context::new(&principal, args).at(request_now.unwrap_or_else(|| shared.now()));
  1231	            if path.starts_with("/q/") {
  1232	                let provenance = payload.get("provenance").and_then(Json::as_bool) == Some(true);
  1233	                if provenance { context = context.with_row_provenance(); }
  1234	                if kind != ProgramKind::Query {
  1235	                    operation_refusal(request, shared, 400, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("{op} is a mutation; POST /m/{op}")));
  1236	                    return;
  1237	                }
  1238	                let retained: Option<Vec<String>> = match payload.get("searchRetained") {
  1239	                    Some(value) => match serde_json::from_value(value.clone()) {
  1240	                        Ok(ids) => Some(ids),
  1241	                        Err(_) => return operation_refusal(request, shared, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "searchRetained must be a page of IDs")),
  1242	                    },
  1243	                    None => None,
  1244	                };
  1245	                if let Some(value) = payload.get("searchRetainedOrder") {
  1246	                    let order: Vec<i64> = match value.as_array().and_then(|values| values.iter().map(snapback4_core::search_decoration::order_key).collect()) {
  1247	                        Some(order) => order,
  1248	                        None => return operation_refusal(request, shared, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "searchRetainedOrder must contain integer ordering keys")),
  1249	                    };
  1250	                    if retained.as_ref().is_none_or(|ids| ids.len() != order.len()) {
     1	//! Held change polls: JSON whitespace heartbeats, followed by one JSON result.
     2	use super::{
     3	    JsonBuffer, Shared, cors, lock, principal_of, query_params, refusal_json, respond_timed,
     4	    trusted_dev_request,
     5	};
     6	use serde_json::{Value as Json, json};
     7	use std::io;
     8	use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
     9	use tiny_http::Request;
    10	
    11	const HEARTBEAT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(2);
    12	
    13	pub(super) fn serve(request: Request, shared: &Shared, url: &str) {
    14	    let params = query_params(url);
    15	    let since: u64 = params.get("since").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
    16	    let wait: u64 = super::wait_seconds(url);
    17	    let began = Instant::now();
    18	    let deadline = began + Duration::from_secs(wait);
    19	    let paced = began + Duration::from_millis(100);
    20	    let capability = match principal_of(&request, shared) {
    21	        Ok(principal) => principal,
    22	        Err(refusal) => {
    23	            return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&refusal) }));
    24	        }
    25	    };
    26	    if let Err(denied) = crate::device_handshake::check(shared, crate::device_handshake::header(&request), false) {
    27	        return respond_timed(request, shared, 409, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&denied) }));
    28	    }
    29	    let connected = &capability.0;
    30	    // @ref LLP 3000.036#6-idle-live-clients-must-not-cost-a-thread — one principal's tabs cannot take the whole viewer pool; refused at once, never queued.
    31	    let _share = if wait > 0 {
    32	        match shared.long_waits.enter(connected) {
    33	            Ok(share) => Some(share),
    34	            Err(limit) => return super::long_waits::refuse_share(request, shared, limit),
    35	        }
    36	    } else { None };
    37	    let present = capability.2 == snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full;
    38	    let heartbeat = params.get("heartbeat").is_some_and(|s| s == "1") && request.http_version().0 == 1 && request.http_version().1 >= 1;
    39	    let mut response = PollResponse { request: Some(request), started: false, shared };
    40	    let initial = { let wake=lock(&shared.wake); (wake.seq,wake.eph.version.clone(),wake.generation) };
    41	    let epoch = params.get("eph").and_then(|s| s.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(initial.1.epoch);
    42	    let incarnation = params.get("ephi").cloned().unwrap_or(initial.1.incarnation);
    43	    let interest: Option<std::collections::BTreeSet<String>> = params.get("ephTables").map(|s| {
    44	        s.split(',').filter(|s|!s.is_empty()).map(str::to_owned).collect()
    45	    });
    46	    let wants_ephemeral = ["eph", "ephi", "ephTables"].iter().any(|k|params.contains_key(*k));
    47	    {
    48	        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    49	        if let Err(why) = super::session_identity::persona_read_authority(response.request.as_ref().unwrap(), shared, &engine) {
    50	            drop(engine); response.finish(401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)})); return;
    51	        }
    52	        if present && let Err(error) = engine.presence_reconnected(&connected, shared.now()) {
    53	            response.finish(500, json!({ "denied": refusal_json(&error.refusal()) })); return;
    54	        }
    55	        if present { shared.presence.enter(&connected); }
    56	    }
    57	    let result = (|| -> Result<_, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
    58	        // Entry always reads the store: the client may already know a head
    59	        // committed after the last publication (LLP 3000 finding 5).
    60	        let mut current=read_answer(shared, since)?;
    61	        let mut observed=initial.0;
    62	        let mut sequence_wake=false;
    63	        let mut beat=Instant::now();
    64	        loop {
    65	            // The deadline is private scheduling state. Production polls wake
    66	            // at it even without another application request; development
    67	            // clock jumps run the same advancement through their control
    68	            // request and notify this condition variable.
    69	            let opening = if shared.personas {
    70	                None
    71	            } else {
    72	                shared.next_time_audience()?
    73	            };
    74	            // Snapshot and wait share one mutex. Commit cannot pass between
    75	            // the predicate check and the condvar's atomic unlock-and-wait.
    76	            let wake=lock(&shared.wake);
    77	            if wake.seq != observed {
    78	                observed=wake.seq; sequence_wake=true; drop(wake);
    79	                current=answer(shared, since)?;
    80	                continue;
    81	            }
    82	            let tables=wake.eph.changed(epoch,Some(&incarnation),interest.as_ref());
    83	            let reset=wake.eph.version.incarnation!=incarnation;
    84	            let eph_ready=reset||!tables.is_empty();
    85	            let durable=wake.generation != initial.2 || sequence_wake || current.0!=since || params.get("store_id").is_some_and(|id|Some(id)!=current.1.as_ref());
    86	            let now=Instant::now();
    87	            if durable || now>=deadline || eph_ready && now>=paced {
    88	                let eph=snapback4_core::engine::ephemeral::Change {preserved:wake.eph.preserved_since(epoch,&incarnation),epoch:wake.eph.version.epoch,incarnation:wake.eph.version.incarnation.clone(),tables};
    89	                let generation=wake.generation;
    90	                // Replacement publishes its identity with the incarnation. No
    91	                // engine lock is needed to finish an ephemeral-only wake.
    92	                if reset {current.1=wake.store_id.clone();}
    93	                return Ok((current,generation,eph));
    94	            }
    95	            if heartbeat && now>=beat {
    96	                drop(wake); response.heartbeat().map_err(|e|snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store(e.to_string()))?;
    97	                beat=Instant::now()+HEARTBEAT;continue;
    98	            }
    99	            let mut until=if heartbeat {deadline.min(beat)} else {deadline};
   100	            if eph_ready {until=until.min(paced);}
   101	            if let Some(opening) = opening {
   102	                until = until.min(Instant::now() + shared.time_audience_delay(opening));
   103	            }
   104	            #[cfg(test)]
   105	            super::test_work(&shared.poll_wait);
   106	            let (wake, timeout)=shared.polls.wait_timeout(wake,until.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now())).unwrap_or_else(|e|e.into_inner());
   107	            #[cfg(test)]
   108	            super::test_work(&shared.poll_resumed);
   109	            #[cfg(test)]
   110	            if !timeout.timed_out() { super::test_work(&shared.poll_notified); }
   111	            drop(wake);
   112	            if timeout.timed_out()
   113	                && opening.is_some_and(|at| shared.time_audience_delay(at).is_zero())
   114	            {
   115	                shared.advance_time_audiences()?;
   116	                current = answer(shared, since)?;
   117	            }
   118	        }
   119	    })();
   120	    // @ref LLP 3000.033#1.2 — a reader never enters OR leaves presence.
   121	    if present { shared.presence.leave(&connected); }
   122	    // Recheck bearer liveness on a pooled detached reader. Ephemeral-only
   123	    // wakes must not reacquire the serving engine merely to finish a poll.
   124	    let auth_store_id = lock(&shared.wake).store_id.clone();
   125	    let bearer = response.request.as_ref().unwrap().headers().iter().find(|h|h.field.equiv("Authorization")).and_then(|h|crate::auth::parse_bearer(h.value.as_str())).map(|token| token.trim().to_owned());
   126	    let authenticated = match bearer.map(|token| (shared.reader(), token)) {
   127	        None => Ok(()),
   128	        Some((Ok((reader_epoch, reader)), token)) => {
   129	            let resolved = match snapback4_core::identity::resolve_read_only_supervised(reader.store(),&token,snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample { now: shared.now(), supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Acquire) },auth_store_id.as_deref()) {
   130	                Ok(Some(current)) if current == capability => Ok(Ok(())),Ok(_)=>Ok(Err(snapback4_core::identity::diagnostics::refused(reader.store(), &token, shared.now(), trusted_dev_request(response.request.as_ref().unwrap(), shared)))),Err(e)=>Err(e),
   131	            };
   132	            match resolved {
   133	                Ok(outcome) => { lock(&shared.readers).put(&reader_epoch, reader.into_inner()); outcome }
   134	                // As for answers: a reader older than a finishing adoption is
   135	                // not a verdict on the session. Ask again behind the engine.
   136	                Err(_) => {
   137	                    principal_of(response.request.as_ref().unwrap(),shared).and_then(|current| {
   138	                        if current == capability { Ok(()) } else { Err(snapback4_core::Refusal::new("E_AUTH", "auth", "the poll credential changed")) }
   139	                    })
   140	                }
