     1	# Testing: fixtures and the packaged proof kit
     2	
     3	For shared test releases and probe:serve, use `guide staging`: real password users and operator grants on serve. Password-only dev fixture/clock tools require explicit --unsafe-dev-doors; startDev persists unless --memory is explicit.
     4	
     5	Prove backend behavior with fixtures and the rendered UI with the packaged proof kit.
     6	Starters generate frontend:proof and day2:proof, and the kit owns server and browser lifecycles.
     7	
     8	```js
     9	import { startDev, cleanup } from "snapback4/proof";
    10	const dev = await startDev();
    11	try { console.log(dev.url); } finally { await cleanup(() => dev.stop()); }
    12	```
    13	
    14	Raw HTTP starts with JSON envelopes: POST /q/name `{args:{...}}`, POST /m/name
    15	`{id:"stable-intent-id",args:{...}}`, POST /a/name `{id:"stable-action-id",args:{...}}`.
    16	Keep the same id on an ambiguous retry. A mutation/action id identifies the attempt;
    17	`result.id` identifies a returned row. Query data is under `data`, writes under `result`;
    18	actions have their own lifecycle (`guide actions`), not a mutation receipt.
    19	
    20	The generated frontend:proof builds its own dist with build (web) or build:web (Expo web).
    21	After replacing the starter screen, replace its business assertions too.
    22	`init --adapt` previews only and writes nothing; use the printed `--apply` command.
    23	A custom Home has no write scenario: replace the Home assertions; a proof without a write fails.
    24	`day2:proof` uses versions/v1 and versions/v2 (initial source snapshots and contemporary fixtures).
    25	
    26	E_FIXTURE_ALIAS means a saved alias or path could not resolve. Use an earlier case's `save` name with `$name` or `$name.field`; escape a literal with `$$` or `$literal`. An unresolved assertion fails the case; it never compares the alias as literal data.
    27	
    28	E_FIXTURE_TIME means a relative fixture time is malformed or out of range. Use `now` or `now±duration` with positive integral ms/s/m/h/d/w. Relative conversion applies only to typed time leaves; text/JSON strings stay literal.
    29	
    30	## Refusal ladder
    31	
    32	Supply every valid argument when testing authorization: decode (`E_INPUT`)
    33	runs before the body. For a body ordered lookup → write → require, a hidden
    34	asserted lookup refuses `NOT_FOUND`, a readable denied table write refuses
    35	`E_RULE`, and only then can the later `require` emit its code. After decoding,
    36	statements execute in authored order; put a require first to have it decide
    37	first. Nullable hidden lookups return null; assertions use their authored
    38	`else` code. Hidden denied update/delete targets retain NOT_FOUND. Earlier
    39	writes roll back when a later require refuses.
    40	
    41	A query over hidden rows is complete and empty when the pinned scope uniformly
    42	refuses its viewer. A nonuniform scan can still be partial with no visible
    43	rows. Test the complete flag as well as the count; do not expect E_RULE for a
    44	hidden page. For a compiled role workflow see: `guide rule-recipes role-workflow`.
    45	Use `guide client` for client recipes and the proof helper contracts below.
    46	
    47	## Fixtures and cases
    48	
    49	`test` uses ./fixtures; `--fixture dir` overrides it. A fixture directory has
    50	one `<table>.jsonl` per declared table, each line an object with `id` and fields:
    51	
    52	```jsonl
    53	{"id":"board-alice","owner":"dev:alice","title":"Groceries"}
    54	```
    55	
    56	Persona alice is `dev:alice`, never the literal `alice`. References use row ids;
    57	time is Unix milliseconds or `"now"`, `"now-1h"`, `"now+2d"` in typed time fields
    58	(including optional/list time leaves). Durations are positive whole ms/s/m/h/d/w.
    59	Typed time arguments in cases accept the same forms.
    60	One clock captured at test start anchors these values, seed defaults, cases and
    61	the sweep, so timed fixtures do not rot; malformed/overflowing relative times
    62	refuse E_FIXTURE_TIME. Text and JSON fields retain literal strings.
    63	Money/decimal use integer minor units; bytes are hex; optional fields may be absent/null. Parents load before children.
    64	Defaults, unique keys, caps, maintained values and sequences obey normal insert
    65	semantics: omit sequence and engine-maintained columns. A missing parent is
    66	NOT_FOUND; a bad row or unknown table fails the run before any case executes.
    67	
    68	Add `test.cases.jsonl` to that directory. Cases execute in order; writes change
    69	what later cases see. The complete vocabulary is `as`, `op`, `args`, `expect`,
    70	`data`, `match`, `matchSet`, `count`, `save`, `label`; unknown keys fail before any case runs.
    71	
    72	```jsonl
    73	{"as":"alice","op":"addCard","args":{"board":"board-alice","text":"eggs"},"save":"eggs"}
    74	{"as":"alice","op":"renameCard","args":{"card":"$eggs","text":"milk"}}
    75	{"as":"bob","op":"cardsOn","args":{"board":"board-alice"},"count":0}
    76	```
    77	
    78	`save` names an answer: `$eggs` means its row id, `$eggs.text` its field.
    79	A bare id/reference/principal/cursor/asset argument equal to a saved alias refuses
    80	with the `$name` rewrite (including optional/list leaves). Text, enum and JSON
    81	arguments remain literals. A root `$alias` saved as null refuses E_FIXTURE_ALIAS
    82	by name; `$row.optionalField` may be null.
    83	Bare assertion strings (including enum literals) remain literals even if an alias has that name.
    84	Aliases resolve recursively inside args, data and match. Unknown aliases and
    85	missing fields refuse E_FIXTURE_ALIAS by name. `$$text` means literal
    86	`$text`; `{"$literal":"eggs"}` means literal `eggs`, even if saved as an alias.
    87	`data` asserts the exact result; `match` asserts a recursive object subset
    88	(`"match":{"name":"Ash"}` permits an extra id). Arrays retain their exact
    89	length/order, with each element matched recursively; scalars compare exactly.
    90	`matchSet` compares a list without order: `"matchSet":[{"name":"Ash"},{"name":"Oak"}]`
   140	`cwd` override clock/project. Without credentials it reads the dev-only catalog;
   141	`serve` refuses this door. `startDev` also returns `personas`. To authenticate both
   142	reads, pass `{ url: dev.url, persona: dev.personas[0] }`, or `headers` with your
   143	Authorization header. Reload after schema edits. Reset validates rows;
   144	inspect its reply for `denied`. `/seed` and `/dev/reset` accept relative times
   145	directly; `"clock":"reset"` uses the prospective wall clock atomically.
   146	
   147	## An acceptance script over HTTP
   148	
   149	The same kit supports HTTP and browser proofs. HTTP acknowledgement is not React settlement; mutation refusals are `{state:'failed', why}`.
   150	
   151	```js
   152	import assert from "node:assert/strict";
   153	import { startDev, startStatic, withBrowser, fillRN, renderedText,
   154	  waitForFresh, bounded, cleanup } from "snapback4/proof";
   155	
   156	const dev = await startDev({ args: ["--memory"] }); // disposable store; actual runtime URL
   157	let web;
   158	try {
   159	  web = await startStatic("dist");
   160	  await withBrowser({ executablePath: process.env.CHROME }, async context => {
   161	    const page = await context.newPage();
   162	    await page.goto(`${web.url}/?api=${encodeURIComponent(dev.url)}&witness=web`, { waitUntil: "domcontentloaded" });
   163	    await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Continue as a guest", exact: true }).click();
   164	    const title = `Proof ${crypto.randomUUID()}`;
   165	    await fillRN(page.getByLabel("Task", { exact: true }), title);
   166	    const button = page.getByRole("button", { name: "Add", exact: true });
   167	    await button.evaluate((element, title) => window.__snapback4Witness.beginForm("add", {title}, element), title);
   168	    await button.click();
   169	    const receipt = await bounded(page.evaluate(() => window.__snapback4Witness.formResult()), 30000, "write receipt");
   170	    assert.equal(receipt.state, "sent", JSON.stringify(receipt));
   171	    await waitForFresh(renderedText(page, title, {within: page.getByRole("list")}), null);
   172	  });
   173	} finally { await cleanup(() => web?.stop(), () => dev.stop()); }
   174	```
   175	
   176	The kit spawns binaries directly, binds the first free port in PORT_LO..PORT_HI
   177	at run time (or an OS-assigned port with neither set), strips ANSI ready lines,
   178	asserts served identity and owns bounded cleanup. Each child logs separate
   179	stdout/stderr files in `<cwd>/logs` by default. These survive the harness's
   180	TMPDIR cleanup (H6); set `logsDir` outside a source-discovery directory if your
   200	});
   201

    77	pub(super) fn frontend_proof(source: &str, template: Option<&str>, web: bool) -> String {
    78	    // Social keeps account controls on its Profile tab; prove the actual route.
    79	    let mut proof = source.replace("// TEMPLATE_READY", if template == Some("social") {
    80	        "await page.getByText(\"Profile\", { exact: true }).click();"
    81	    } else { "" });
    82	    if template == Some("social") {
    83	        proof = proof.replace("getByLabel(\"Task\"", "getByLabel(\"Display name\"")
    84	            .replace("name: \"Add\"", "name: \"Save profile\"")
    85	            .replace("beginForm(\"add\", { title }", "beginForm(\"saveProfile\", { name: title }");
    86	    } else if template == Some("chat") {
    87	        proof = proof.replace("    const task =", r#"    const viewer = await page.evaluate(() => window.__snapback4Witness.viewer());
    88	    const seeded = await fetch(`${dev.url}/seed`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: JSON.stringify([
    89	      { table: "conversations", row: { id: "proof-room", title: "Proof room", creatorId: viewer } },
    90	      { table: "memberships", row: { id: "proof-member", conversationId: "proof-room", principal: viewer, role: "admin", joinedAt: Date.now() } }
    91	    ]) });
    92	    assert.ok(seeded.ok, await seeded.text());
    93	    await page.getByText("Proof room", { exact: true }).click();
    94	    const task ="#)
    95	            .replace("page.getByLabel(\"Task\", { exact: true })", "page.getByPlaceholder(\"Message\", { exact: true })")
    96	            .replace("name: \"Add\"", "name: \"Send\"")
    97	            .replace("beginForm(\"add\", { title }", "beginForm(\"send\", { conversationId: \"proof-room\", body: title }");
    98	        // Chat receipts echo drafts. Restrict evidence to its message display.
    99	        if web { proof = proof.replace("renderedText(page, new RegExp(title))", "renderedText(page, new RegExp(title), { within: page.getByRole(\"region\", { name: \"Thread\" }) })"); }
   100	    }
   101	    let scope = match (template, web) {
   102	        (Some("social"), _) => "page.getByTestId(\"profile-results\")",
   103	        (Some("chat"), true) => "page.getByRole(\"region\", { name: \"Thread\" })",
   104	        (Some("chat"), false) => "page.getByTestId(\"thread-results\")",
   105	        (_, true) => "page.getByRole(\"list\")",
   106	        (_, false) => "page.getByTestId(\"todo-results\")",
   107	    };
   108	    proof = proof.replace("RESULTS_SCOPE", scope);
   109	    proof = proof.replace("BUILD_SCRIPT", if web { "build" } else { "build:web" });
   110	    proof
   111	}

   113	fn previous_frontend(dir: &std::path::Path, web: bool) -> Option<Option<&'static str>> {
   114	    super::proof_advice::frontend_template(dir, web).filter(|(version,_)| matches!(*version,"0.4.28"|"0.4.29"|"0.4.30"|"0.4.31")).map(|(_,template)|template)
   115	}

   117	/// Adapt adds missing proof infrastructure, preserving files and script names
   118	/// already owned by the application. It never runs a package manager.
   119	pub(super) fn plan(dir: &std::path::Path, own: &super::OwnSchema, enable_day2: bool) -> std::io::Result<Vec<(&'static str, String)>> {
   120	    let web = dir.join("src/App.tsx").is_file();
   121	    let router = dir.join("app/_layout.tsx").is_file();
   122	    if !web && !dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() && !router { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
   123	    let shape = super::starter::Shape { title: super::title(dir), password: own.identity.is_some_and(|(p, g)| p && !g),
   124	        personas: own.identity.is_none().then(|| own.personas.clone()).filter(|p| !p.is_empty()),
   125	        body: super::starter::Body::Home(None), offline: own.offline.clone() };
   126	    let path = dir.join("package.json");
   127	    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&path)?;
   128	    let mut package: Value = serde_json::from_str(&text).map_err(std::io::Error::other)?;
   129	    let router = router && !web && package["main"] == "expo-router/entry";
   130	    if !web && !dir.join("App.tsx").is_file() && !router { return Ok(Vec::new()); }
   131	    let original = package.clone();
   132	    let day2_text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/day2-proof.mjs")).ok();
   133	    let referenced_day2 = package["scripts"].as_object().is_some_and(|scripts| scripts.values().any(|v| v.as_str().is_some_and(|s| s.contains("scripts/day2-proof.mjs"))));
   134	    let missing_managed_day2 = day2_text.is_none() && matches!(package["scripts"]["day2:proof"].as_str(),
   135	        Some("node scripts/day2-proof.mjs" | "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1.q versions/v2.q" | "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1 versions/v2"));
   136	    let managed_day2 = day2_text.as_deref().is_some_and(super::proof_advice::known_day2) || missing_managed_day2;
   137	    let day2 = enable_day2 || referenced_day2 || package.pointer("/snapback4/starter/day2") != Some(&json!(false)) && managed_day2;
   138	    // Deleting both the command and file records a durable opt-out on apply.
   139	    // Preview/check never write the inferred choice. Explicit --day2 opts in again.
   140	    if (package["snapback4"].is_null() || package["snapback4"].is_object()) && (package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_null() || package["snapback4"]["starter"].is_object()) {
   141	        package["snapback4"]["starter"]["day2"] = json!(day2);
   142	    }
   143	    let backend = package["scripts"]["backend"].as_str().and_then(|s| s.strip_prefix("snapback4 dev --port ")).and_then(|s| s.parse::<u16>().ok())
   144	.or_else(|| launcher_port(dir, "backend")).unwrap_or(4400);
   145	    let frontend = launcher_port(dir, "frontend").or_else(|| package["scripts"][if web { "dev" } else { "start" }].as_str()
   146	        .and_then(|s| s.split_once("--port ")).and_then(|(_, rest)| rest.split_whitespace().next()).and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())
   147	        .or_else(|| std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("vite.config.mts")).ok().and_then(|s| s.split_once("server: { port: ").and_then(|(_, rest)| rest.split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit()).next()).and_then(|n| n.parse::<u16>().ok())))
   148	        ).unwrap_or(if backend != 4400 { backend.saturating_add(1) } else if web { 5173 } else { 8081 });
   149	    let mut generated = vec![("package.json", pretty(&package))];
   150	    let mut flags = std::collections::BTreeMap::new();
   151	    if router { flags.insert("template".into(), "social".into()); }
   152	    if day2 { flags.insert("day2".into(), String::new()); }
   153	    files(&mut generated, &Options { positionals: vec![dir.to_string_lossy().into_owned()], flags, json: false }, backend, frontend, web, &shape);
   154	    // A saved version is immutable application evidence. Never fill its missing
   155	    // fixture files from today's working release during an adaptation.
   156	    generated.retain(|(name, _)| !name.starts_with("versions/") || {
   157	        let version = name.split('/').nth(1).unwrap();
   158	        !dir.join("versions").join(version).exists()
   159	    });
   160	    // New directories capture the current source and contemporary fixtures. Flat
   161	    // legacy snapshots remain untouched evidence; they are never propagated.
   162	    // Custom proofs own their saved source layout; do not manufacture snapshots.
   163	    if !managed_day2 && !enable_day2 { generated.retain(|(name,_)| !name.starts_with("versions/")); }
   164	    let wanted: Value = serde_json::from_str(&generated[0].1).unwrap();
   165	    for group in ["scripts", "devDependencies"] {
   166	        if !package[group].is_object() { package[group] = json!({}); }
   167	        for (key, value) in wanted[group].as_object().unwrap() {
   168	            let old = package[group].get(key).and_then(Value::as_str);
   169	            let managed = group == "scripts" && old.is_some_and(|old| match key.as_str() {
   170	                "day2:proof" => managed_day2 && matches!(old, "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs" | "node scripts/day2-proof.mjs versions/v1.q versions/v2.q"),
   171	                "backend" => old.strip_prefix("snapback4 dev --port ").is_some_and(|n| n.parse::<u16>().is_ok()),
   172	                "dev" | "preview" => old.strip_prefix(if key == "dev" { "vite --port " } else { "vite preview --port " }).and_then(|s| s.strip_suffix(" --strictPort")).is_some_and(|n| n.parse::<u16>().is_ok()),
   173	                "start" | "web" => old.strip_prefix(if key == "start" { "expo start --port " } else { "expo start --web --port " }).is_some_and(|n| n.parse::<u16>().is_ok()),
   174	                _ => false,
   175	            });
   176	            if managed || ((key == "day2:proof" && enable_day2 || key == "frontend:proof" && super::proof_advice::known_frontend(dir) || key == "playwright-core" && (super::proof_advice::known_frontend(dir) || super::proof_advice::referenced(dir, "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs"))) && old.is_none()) { package[group][key] = value.clone(); }
   177	        }
   178	    }
   179	    let mut config_changes = Vec::new();
   180	    if web {
   181	        if let Ok(current) = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("vite.config.mts")) {
   182	            // Replace only the exact initializer forms, allowing their port literals.
   183	            let numbers = current.split("port: ").skip(1).filter_map(|s| s.split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_digit()).next()?.parse::<u16>().ok()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   184	            let mut normalized = current.clone();
   185	            for number in &numbers { normalized = normalized.replace(&format!("port: {number}"), "port: 5173"); }
   186	            let reference = include_str!("starter/web/vite.config.mts").replace("port: 4173", "port: 5173");
   187	            if normalized == reference { config_changes.push(("vite.config.mts", runtime_vite(&current, backend, numbers.first().copied().unwrap_or(5173)))); }
   188	        }
   189	    }
   190	    let inherited_frontend = previous_frontend(dir, web);
   191	    if let Some(template) = inherited_frontend {
   192	        if let Some((_, text)) = generated.iter_mut().find(|(name, _)| *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs") {
   193	            *text = frontend_proof(include_str!("frontend-proof.mjs"), template, web);
   194	        }
   195	    }
   196	    let referenced_after = |path: &str| package["scripts"].as_object().is_some_and(|scripts| scripts.values().any(|v|v.as_str().is_some_and(|s|s.contains(path))));
   197	    let mut additions: Vec<_> = generated.into_iter().skip(1)
   198	        .filter(|(name, _)| {
   199	            if *name == "scripts/frontend-proof.mjs" { return inherited_frontend.is_some() || !dir.join(name).exists() && referenced_after(name); }
   200	            if *name == "scripts/day2-proof.mjs" { return day2 && (managed_day2 || !dir.join(name).exists() && (enable_day2 || referenced_day2)); }
   201	            if !dir.join(name).exists() {
   202	                return name.starts_with("versions/") || referenced_after(name);
   203	            }
   204	            false
   205	        }).collect();
   206	    additions.extend(config_changes);
   207	    if package != original { additions.push(("package.json", pretty(&package))); }
   208	    Ok(additions)
   209	}

     7	// For SVG/foreignObject controls, click the actual rendered box, not a text

     9	async function clickSvg(page, control) {
    10	  await control.waitFor({ state: "visible" });
    11	  const box = await control.boundingBox();
    12	  assert.ok(box && box.width > 0 && box.height > 0, "SVG control has no rendered box");
    13	  await page.mouse.click(box.x + box.width / 2, box.y + box.height / 2);
    14	}

    20	assert.equal(build.code, 0, `frontend:proof build failed: ${build.stderr}; logs: ${build.logs.stderr}`);

    26	  web = await startStatic("dist", options);

    28	    const page = await context.newPage();

    30	    if (identity.guests) {

    34	      await fillRN(page.getByLabel("Password", { exact: true }), "Proof-password-928!");
    35	      await page.getByRole("button", { name: "Sign up", exact: true }).click();
    36	    } else {

    56	    }
    57	  });

    59	} finally { await cleanup(() => web?.stop(), () => dev.stop()); }
     1	// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#1 — direct children, file logs and bounded cleanup own proof orchestration.
     2	import { spawn, type ChildProcess } from "node:child_process";
     3	import { closeSync, mkdirSync, openSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from "node:fs";
     4	import { join, resolve } from "node:path";
     5	import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
     6	import { setTimeout as sleep } from "node:timers/promises";

     9	const children = new Set<ChildProcess>();
    10	process.once("exit", () => { for (const child of children) child.kill("SIGKILL"); });
    11	export function register(child: ChildProcess): void {
    12	  children.add(child);
    13	  child.once("exit", () => children.delete(child));
    14	  child.on("error", () => children.delete(child));
    15	}
    16	export interface ProofOptions {
    17	  cwd?: string;
    18	  env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv;
    19	  args?: readonly string[];
    20	  timeoutMs?: number;
    21	  stopTimeoutMs?: number;
    22	  logsDir?: string;
    23	  /** Keep stdout in memory only (stderr remains diagnostic evidence). */
    24	  secret?: boolean;
    25	  /** Internal: a frontend keeper owns this private process group. */
    26	  detached?: boolean;
    27	  /** Internal: the frontend observes parent death through stdin EOF. */
    28	  livenessPipe?: boolean;
    29	}
    30	export interface ProofHost {
    31	  url: string;
    32	  pid: number;
    33	  logs: { stdout: string; stderr: string };
    34	  stop(): Promise<void>;
    35	}
    36	export function deadline(ms: number | undefined, fallback: number): number {
    37	  const value = ms ?? fallback;
    38	  if (!Number.isFinite(value) || value < 1) throw new RangeError("proof timeout must be positive and finite");
    39	  return value;
    40	}
    41	export async function bounded<T>(work: Promise<T>, ms: number, label: string): Promise<T> {
    42	  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
    43	  try {
    44	    return await Promise.race([work, new Promise<never>((_, reject) => {
    45	      timer = setTimeout(() => reject(Error(`${label} timed out after ${ms} ms`)), ms);
    46	    })]);
    47	  } finally { clearTimeout(timer); }
    48	}
    49	/** Attempts every cleanup, even if a preceding step fails or stalls. */
    50	export async function cleanup(...steps: Array<(() => unknown | Promise<unknown>) | undefined>): Promise<void> {
    51	  const errors: unknown[] = [];
    52	  for (const step of steps) if (step) {
    53	    try { await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(step), 10_000, "proof cleanup"); }
    54	    catch (error) { errors.push(error); }
    55	  }
    56	  if (errors.length) throw new AggregateError(errors, "proof cleanup failed");
    57	}
    58	export async function stopChild(child: ChildProcess | Pick<ProofHost, "url" | "stop">, graceMs = 5000): Promise<void> {
    59	  deadline(graceMs, 5000);
    60	  if (child && "url" in child && typeof child.url === "string" && typeof child.stop === "function") {
    61	    await bounded(Promise.resolve().then(() => child.stop()), graceMs + 5000, "host stop"); return;
    62	  }
    63	  if (!child || !("kill" in child) || typeof child.kill !== "function" || typeof child.once !== "function"
    64	      || !(child.exitCode === null || typeof child.exitCode === "number")
    65	      || !(child.signalCode === null || typeof child.signalCode === "string")) {
    66	    throw new TypeError("stopChild needs a ChildProcess or { url, stop }");
    67	  }
    68	  if (child.exitCode !== null || child.signalCode !== null) return;
    69	  let done!: () => void;
    70	  const exited = new Promise<void>(resolve => { done = resolve; });
    71	  child.once("exit", done);
    72	  child.once("error", done);
    73	  try {
    74	    child.kill("SIGTERM");
    75	    try { await bounded(exited, graceMs, "SIGTERM"); }
    76	    catch {
    77	      child.kill("SIGKILL");
    78	      await bounded(exited, 5000, "SIGKILL reap");
    79	    }
    80	  } finally { child.removeListener("exit", done); child.removeListener("error", done); }
    81	}
    82	export function portRange(env: NodeJS.ProcessEnv = process.env): string {
    83	  const low = env.PORT_LO, high = env.PORT_HI;
    84	  if (low === undefined && high === undefined) return "0";
    85	  if (!low || !high || !/^\d+$/.test(low) || !/^\d+$/.test(high)
    86	      || Number(low) < 1 || Number(high) > 65535 || Number(low) > Number(high)) {
    87	    throw Error("PORT_LO and PORT_HI must both specify an inclusive range, 1 <= low <= high <= 65535");
    88	  }
    89	  return `${Number(low)}-${Number(high)}`;
    90	}
    91	/** App-local evidence survives harness cleanup of its temporary root. */
    92	export function logsDir(options: Pick<ProofOptions, "cwd" | "logsDir"> = {}): string {
    93	  return resolve(options.cwd ?? ".", options.logsDir ?? "logs");
    94	}
    95	export function logged(command: string, args: readonly string[], options: ProofOptions = {}) {
    96	  const cwd = resolve(options.cwd ?? "."), id = randomUUID();
    97	  const directory = logsDir(options); mkdirSync(directory, { recursive: true, mode: 0o700 });
    98	  const logs = { stdout: join(directory, `${id}.stdout.log`), stderr: join(directory, `${id}.stderr.log`) };
    99	  if (options.secret) writeFileSync(logs.stdout, "stdout withheld: secret command (returned only to caller)\n", { flag: "wx", mode: 0o600 });
   100	  const out = options.secret ? undefined : openSync(logs.stdout, "wx", 0o600), err = openSync(logs.stderr, "wx", 0o600);
   101	  let child: ChildProcess;
   102	  try { child = spawn(command, [...args], { cwd, detached: options.detached ?? false, env: { ...(options.env ?? process.env), SNAPBACK4_PROOF_LAUNCH: id }, stdio: [options.livenessPipe ? "pipe" : "ignore", options.secret ? "pipe" : out!, err] }); }
   103	  finally { if (out !== undefined) closeSync(out); closeSync(err); }
   104	  // Secret stdout never reaches a file, even briefly. Callers still receive it.
   105	  let stdout = "";
   106	  child.stdout?.setEncoding("utf8");
   107	  child.stdout?.on("data", chunk => { stdout += chunk; });
   108	  register(child);
   109	  child.stdin?.on("error", () => {});
   110	  let error: Error | undefined;
   111	  child.on("error", value => { error = value; });
   112	  const closed = new Promise<void>(resolve => child.once("close", () => resolve()));
   113	  return { child, logs, id, closed, stdout: () => options.secret ? stdout : logText(logs.stdout), error: () => error };
   114	}
   115	export const logText = (path: string): string => readFileSync(path, "utf8");
   116	export async function ready(run: ReturnType<typeof logged>, parse: (line: string) => string | undefined, timeoutMs: number): Promise<string> {
   117	  const end = performance.now() + timeoutMs;
   118	  let outAt = 0, errAt = 0;
   119	  while (performance.now() < end) {
   120	    if (run.error()) throw run.error();
   121	    if (run.child.exitCode !== null || run.child.signalCode !== null) {
   122	      throw Error(`proof child exited before ready (${run.child.exitCode ?? run.child.signalCode}); ${run.logs.stderr}\n${logText(run.logs.stderr).slice(-4000)}`);
   123	    }
   124	    for (const [path, offset, stream] of [[run.logs.stdout, outAt, "out"], [run.logs.stderr, errAt, "err"]] as const) {
   125	      const text = logText(path);
   126	      const complete = text.lastIndexOf("\n") + 1;
   127	      for (const line of text.slice(offset, complete).split("\n")) {
   128	        const url = parse(stripVTControlCharacters(line).trim());
   129	        if (url) return url;
   130	      }
   131	      if (stream === "out") outAt = complete; else errAt = complete;
   132	    }
   133	    await sleep(25);
   134	  }
   135	  throw Error(`proof child ready timed out after ${timeoutMs} ms; logs: ${run.logs.stdout}, ${run.logs.stderr}`);
   136	}

     1	//! Read checkout is independent of the schema-adoption writer.
     2	use super::*;
     3	
     4	// @ref LLP 3000#7-decisions-during-execution — reader reuse retains fresh transactions and retires old native modules.
     5	/// Reuse only detached read owners. Each query still opens and rolls back
     6	/// its own identity-checked SQLite transaction; no result or viewer is cached.
     7	#[derive(Default)]
     8	pub(super) struct QueryReaders {
     9	    pub(super) epoch: Arc<()>,
    10	    pub(super) idle: Vec<Engine>,
    11	    pub(super) source: Option<Engine>,
    12	    memory: bool,
    13	    pub(super) refusal_sites: Arc<dev_refusals::Sites>,
    14	    #[cfg(test)]
    15	    pub(super) fail_open_after: Option<usize>,
    16	    #[cfg(test)]
    17	    pub(super) retry_open: Option<Arc<dyn Fn() + Send + Sync>>,
    18	    candidate_queries: BTreeSet<String>,
    19	    launching_feed: bool,
    20	}
    21	
    22	impl QueryReaders {
    23	    pub(super) fn take(
    24	        &mut self,
    25	        engine: &Engine,
    26	    ) -> Result<(Arc<()>, Engine), snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
    27	        self.memory = engine.store().conn().path().is_none_or(str::is_empty);
    28	        if self.source.is_none() {
    29	            self.source = engine.reader().ok();
    30	        }
    31	        let reader = match self.idle.pop() {
    32	            Some(reader) => reader,
    33	            None => engine.reader()?,
    34	        };
    35	        Ok((self.epoch.clone(), reader))
    36	    }
    37	
    38	    pub(super) fn put(&mut self, epoch: &Arc<()>, reader: Engine) {
    39	        if Arc::ptr_eq(epoch, &self.epoch) && self.idle.len() < REQUEST_WORKERS {
    40	            self.idle.push(reader);
    41	        }
    42	    }
    43	
    44	    pub(super) fn invalidate(&mut self) {
    45	        // Native code can change without a schema-generation change. Loans
    46	        // retain their old epoch so an in-flight return cannot repopulate it.
    47	        self.epoch = Arc::new(());
    48	        self.idle.clear();
    49	        self.source = None;
    50	        self.candidate_queries.clear();
    51	        self.launching_feed = false;
    52	    }
    53	}
    54	
    55	impl QueryReaders {
    56	    pub(super) fn new(engine: &Engine, sites: &dev_refusals::Sites) -> Self {
    57	        Self {
    58	            source: engine.reader().ok(),
    59	            refusal_sites: Arc::new(sites.clone()),
    60	            memory: engine.store().conn().path().is_none_or(str::is_empty),
    61	            ..Self::default()
    62	        }
    63	    }
    64	    pub(super) fn replace(&mut self, engine: &Engine, sites: &dev_refusals::Sites) {
    65	        self.invalidate();
    66	        self.refusal_sites = Arc::new(sites.clone());
    67	        self.memory = engine.store().conn().path().is_none_or(str::is_empty);
    68	        self.source = engine.reader().ok();
    69	    }
    70	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — retry a failed factory on existing writer work; reads still refuse immediately.
    71	    pub(super) fn retry_missing(&mut self, engine: &Engine) {
    72	        if !self.memory && self.source.is_none() {
    73	            self.source = latency::acquire(|| {
    74	                #[cfg(test)]
    75	                if let Some(open) = &self.retry_open { open(); }
    76	                engine.reader()
    77	            }).ok();
    78	        }
    79	    }
    80	    pub(super) fn begin_adoption(&mut self, candidate: &Bound) {
    81	        self.launching_feed = self.source.as_ref().is_some_and(|old| candidate.schema.timelines.iter()
    82	            .any(|next| !old.schema().timelines.iter().any(|t| t.same_declaration(next))));
    83	        self.candidate_queries = candidate
    84	            .programs
    85	            .iter()
    86	            .filter(|p| p.program.kind == ProgramKind::Query)
    87	            .map(|p| p.program.name.clone())
    88	            .collect();
    89	    }
    90	    pub(super) fn finish_adoption(&mut self) {
    91	        self.candidate_queries.clear();
    92	        self.launching_feed = false;
    93	    }
    94	    pub(super) fn launching_feed(&self) -> bool { self.launching_feed }
    95	    pub(super) fn pending(&self, name: &str) -> bool {
    96	        self.candidate_queries.contains(name)
    97	    }
    98	    fn detached(&mut self) -> Option<Result<(Arc<()>, Engine), snapback4_core::RuntimeError>> {
    99	        #[cfg(test)]
   100	        if let Some(remaining) = &mut self.fail_open_after {
   101	            if *remaining == 0 {
   102	                self.idle.clear();
   103	                return Some(Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store("injected reader-open failure; retry the request".into())));
   104	            }
   105	            *remaining -= 1;
   106	        }
   107	        let source = self.source.as_ref()?;
   108	        Some(match self.idle.pop() {
   109	            Some(reader) => Ok((self.epoch.clone(), reader)),
   110	            None => source.reader().map(|reader| (self.epoch.clone(), reader)),
   111	        })
   112	    }
   113	}
   114	impl Shared {
   115	    // @ref LLP 3000.019#3.6 — a fresh WAL snapshot reads the old program during atomic activation.
   116	    // The source is only a connection factory: no transaction, answer or viewer
   117	    // is retained. Identity guards still refuse an obsolete generation.
   118	    pub(super) fn reader(&self) -> Result<(Arc<()>, TimedReader), snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
   119	        let (result, sites) = self.acquire_reader(|| {
   120	            let start = trace::begin();
   121	            let mut readers = lock(&self.readers);
   122	            trace::waited(start, "readers");
   123	            (readers.detached(), readers.refusal_sites.clone())
   124	        });
   125	        if let Some(result) = result {
   126	            return result.map(|(epoch, reader)| (epoch, TimedReader { engine: reader, refusal_sites: sites, _timing: latency::EngineSpan::new() }));
   127	        }
   128	        // A missing factory can also mean opening a file-backed reader failed.
   129	        // Never acquire the writer to repair that failure under a read permit.
   130	        Err(snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Store("detached reader unavailable; retry the request after the store is available".into()))
   131	    }
   132	    fn acquire_reader<T>(&self, open: impl FnOnce() -> T) -> T {
   133	        latency::acquire(|| {
   134	            #[cfg(test)]
   135	            test_work(&self.reader_open);
   136	            open()
   137	        })
   138	    }
   139	    pub(super) fn open_reader(&self, engine: &Engine) -> Result<Engine, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
   140	        self.acquire_reader(|| engine.reader())
   141	    }
   142	    pub(super) fn take_reader(&self, engine: &Engine) -> Result<(Arc<()>, Engine), snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
   143	        self.acquire_reader(|| lock(&self.readers).take(engine))
   144	    }
   145	    pub(super) fn memory_store(&self) -> bool { lock(&self.readers).memory }
   146	
   147	    // Identity, head and generation are captured from one reader incarnation.
   148	    pub(super) fn read_identity(&self) -> Result<(String, u64, u64), Refusal> {
   149	        match self.reader() {
   150	            Ok((epoch, mut reader)) => {
   151	                let generation = reader.generation();
   152	                let head = response_head(&mut reader).map(|(id, head)| (id, head, generation));
   153	                lock(&self.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
   154	                head
   155	            }
   156	            Err(_) if self.memory_store() => {
   157	                let mut engine = lock(&self.engine);
   158	                let generation = engine.generation();
   159	                response_head(&mut engine).map(|(id, head)| (id, head, generation))
   160	            }
   161	            Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
   162	        }
   163	    }
   164	
   165	    pub(super) fn read_head(&self) -> Result<(String, u64), Refusal> {
   166	        match self.reader() {
   167	            Ok((epoch, mut reader)) => {
   168	                let head = response_head(&mut reader);
   169	                lock(&self.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner());
   170	                head
   171	            }
   172	            Err(_) if self.memory_store() => response_head(&mut lock(&self.engine)),
   173	            Err(error) => Err(error.refusal()),
   174	        }
   175	    }
   176	}
   177	
   178	/// `--fresh` still dies with its host, but a private WAL file permits detached
   179	/// readers during a schema save. Only this guard removes its own directory.
   180	pub(super) struct FreshDirectory(std::path::PathBuf);
   181	impl FreshDirectory {
   182	    pub(super) fn new() -> Result<Self, String> {
   183	        let id =
   184	            snapback4_core::ids::mint(snapback4_core::now_ms(), &mut snapback4_core::ids::Mint::default())
   185	                .map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   186	        let path =
   187	            std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("snapback4-fresh-{}-{id}", std::process::id()));
   188	        std::fs::create_dir(&path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   189	        let owned = Self(path);
   190	        snapback4_core::private_fs::directory(&owned.0).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
   191	        Ok(owned)
   192	    }
   193	    pub(super) fn store(&self) -> Result<snapback4_core::Store, snapback4_core::RuntimeError> {
   194	        snapback4_core::Store::open(&self.0.join("store.db"))
   195	    }
   196	}
   197	impl Drop for FreshDirectory {
   198	    fn drop(&mut self) {
   199	        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.0);
   200	    }
   201	}
   202	
   203	// Ownership ends on return to the pool or on an error/drop (including sync).
   204	pub(super) struct TimedReader { engine: Engine, pub(super) refusal_sites: Arc<dev_refusals::Sites>, _timing: latency::EngineSpan }
   205	impl TimedReader {
   206	    pub(super) fn into_inner(self) -> Engine { self.engine }
   207	}
   208	impl std::ops::Deref for TimedReader {
   209	    type Target = Engine;
   210	    fn deref(&self) -> &Engine { &self.engine }
   211	}
   212	impl std::ops::DerefMut for TimedReader {
   213	    fn deref_mut(&mut self) -> &mut Engine { &mut self.engine }
   214	}
  1250	                    if retained.as_ref().is_none_or(|ids| ids.len() != order.len()) {
  1251	                        return operation_refusal(request, shared, 400, Refusal::new("E_INPUT", "input", "retained search ordering keys require matching candidate IDs"));
  1252	                    }
  1253	                    context.search_retained_order = Some((order, payload.get("searchRetainedMore").and_then(Json::as_bool).unwrap_or(false)));
  1254	                }
  1255	                let settlement_started = Instant::now();
  1256	                let (outcome, cut, metadata, reads_presence, query_generation, search_current, scopes, decoration_paths) = loop {
  1257	                let reader = shared.reader();
  1258	                let evaluated = match reader {
  1259	                    Ok((epoch, mut reader)) => {
  1260	                        let metadata = response_head(&mut reader);
  1261	                        #[cfg(test)] test_work(&shared.query_wait);
  1262	                        if let Err(why) = session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &reader) {
  1263	                            return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
  1264	                        }

  1266	                        // never from an engine that may have replaced it meanwhile.
  1267	                        let reads_presence = presence_of(&reader);
  1268	                        let context = dev_refusals::response::context(context.clone(), reader.refusal_sites.clone(), &reader);
  1269	                        let query_generation = reader.generation();
  1270	                        let decoration_paths = reader.program(&op).filter(|_| reader.schema().operations.get(&op).is_some_and(|f| f.search_decoration)).map(snapback4_core::search_decoration::paths);
  1271	                        let scopes = read_scopes::dependencies(&reader, &op, &context);
  1272	                        trace::mark("query_start");

  1274	                        trace::mark("query_end");

  1276	                        if outcome.is_ok() { lock(&shared.readers).put(&epoch, reader.into_inner()); }
  1277	                        (outcome, cut, metadata, reads_presence, query_generation, current, scopes, decoration_paths)
  1278	                    },
  1279	                    // Only memory stores lack detached readers by construction.
  1280	                    Err(error) if !shared.memory_store() => {
  1281	                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied": error.refusal()}));
  1282	                    }
  1283	                    Err(_) => {
  1284	                        let mut engine = lock(&shared.engine);
  1285	                        if let Err(why) = session_identity::persona_read_authority(&request, shared, &engine) {
  1286	                            drop(engine); return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&why)}));
  1287	                        }
  1288	                        let metadata = response_head(&mut engine);
  1289	                        let reads_presence = presence_of(&engine);
  1290	                        let mut context = context.clone();
  1291	                        if shared.personas { context = dev_refusals::response::context(context, Arc::new(lock(&shared.bound).refusal_sites.clone()), &engine); }
  1292	                        let query_generation = engine.generation();
  1293	                        let decoration_paths = engine.program(&op).filter(|_| engine.schema().operations.get(&op).is_some_and(|f| f.search_decoration)).map(snapback4_core::search_decoration::paths);
  1294	                        let scopes = read_scopes::dependencies(&engine, &op, &context);
  1295	                        let (outcome, cut, current) = engine.query_materialized_search_with_delivery_at(&op, context, retained.as_deref(), manifest);
  1296	                        let scopes = read_scopes::complete(scopes, engine.schema(), outcome.as_ref().map_or(&[], |o| &o.tables));
  1297	                        (outcome, cut, metadata, reads_presence, query_generation, current, scopes, decoration_paths)
  1298	                    },
  1299	                };
  1300	                if let Some((table, id)) = evaluated.0.as_ref().ok().and_then(|outcome| outcome.pending_opening.as_ref()) {
  1301	                    if settlement_started.elapsed() >= Duration::from_secs(30) || crate::lifecycle::stopping() {
  1302	                        let mut why = Refusal::new("E_QUERY_RETRY", "client", "timed disclosure did not settle within the query wait; retry the query").guide("client");
  1303	                        why.retryable = true;
  1304	                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied":why}));
  1305	                    }

  1307	                    let now = match engine.materialize_time_audience_at(table, id, context.now.unwrap_or_else(|| shared.now())) {
  1308	                        Ok(now) => now,
  1309	                        Err(error) => return respond_timed(request, shared, 503, json!({"denied":error.refusal()})),
  1310	                    };

  1312	                    shared.durable_committed(&engine);

  1315	                        return respond_timed(request, shared, 401, json!({"denied":why}));
  1316	                    }
  1317	                    std::thread::yield_now();
  1318	                    continue;
  1319	                }
  1320	                break evaluated;

  1325	                        #[derive(serde::Serialize)]

  1361	                        impl latency::Reply for QueryReply<'_> { fn generation(&self)->Option<u64>{Some(self.generation)} }
  1362	                        // @ref LLP 3000.018#3 — an answer of a query that may read presence asks its client to re-run it: no change feed names presence.
  1363	                        let refresh = reads_presence.then(|| PRESENCE_REFRESH.as_millis() as u64);

  1383	                    }

  1387	                        // taken before it may predate, so a client can order it against a remembered answer.
  1388	                        if let Some(cut) = cut { reply["seq"] = json!(cut); }

  1390	                        if reads_presence && !refusal.retryable { reply["refresh"] = json!(PRESENCE_REFRESH.as_millis() as u64); }
  1391	                        respond_timed(request, shared, if refusal.retryable { 503 } else if refusal.code=="E_INPUT" && refusal.family=="input" {400} else {200}, reply);
  1392	                    }
  1393	                }
  1394	            } else {
  1395	                if kind == ProgramKind::Query {
  1396	                    operation_refusal(request, shared, 400, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("{op} is a query; POST /q/{op}")));
  1397	                    return;
  1398	                }
  1399	                if kind == ProgramKind::Job {
  1400	                    operation_refusal(request, shared, 400, Refusal::new("E_OP", "op", format!("{op} is a job: a schedule fires it, or `snapback4 run {op}` does (in dev also POST /dev/jobs/{op} with {{\"args\":{{..}}}}); no client call can")).guide("jobs"));

  1402	                }

  1406	                }

  1443	        (Method::Get, "/changes") => changes::serve(request, shared, &url),
  1444	        (Method::Post, "/sync") => sync::serve(request, shared, &body, request_now.expect("sync pins request time")),
  1445	        (Method::Post, "/auth/session") => {
  1446	            // An application that authenticates its own accounts (a phone
  1447	            // it verified, a directory it owns) mints their sessions through
  1448	            // its service and hands each token to that account's device.
  1449	            let payload: Json = serde_json::from_str(if body.is_empty() { "{}" } else { &body }).unwrap_or(Json::Null);
  1450	            let wire = session_mint_wire(&request, &payload);
     1	//! Advice is evidence about known template bytes, or explicitly a heuristic.
     2	// @ref LLP 3000.011.003.000.000#11-wave-14-truthful-starter-custody-day4 — custom proofs own their arguments and assertions.
     3	use std::path::Path;
     4	use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
     5	use serde::Deserialize;
     6	
     7	#[derive(Deserialize)]
     8	struct Template { file: String, version: String, template: Option<String>, web: bool, sha256: String }
     9	fn templates() -> &'static [Template] {
    10	    static TEMPLATES: std::sync::OnceLock<Vec<Template>> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
    11	    TEMPLATES.get_or_init(|| serde_json::from_str(include_str!("proof-digests.json")).expect("shipped proof digests"))
    12	}
    13	fn digest(text: &str) -> String { format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(text.as_bytes())) }
    14	pub(super) fn known_day2(text: &str) -> bool {
    15	    let hash = digest(text);
    16	    templates().iter().any(|t| t.file == "day2" && t.sha256 == hash)
    17	}
    18	pub(super) fn frontend_template(dir: &Path, web: bool) -> Option<(&'static str, Option<&'static str>)> {
    19	    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs")).ok()?;
    20	    let hash = digest(&text);
    21	    templates().iter().find(|t| t.file == "frontend" && t.web == web && t.sha256 == hash)
    22	        .map(|t| (t.version.as_str(), t.template.as_deref()))
    23	}
    24	pub(super) fn known_frontend(dir: &Path) -> bool { [false,true].into_iter().any(|web|frontend_template(dir,web).is_some()) }
    25	fn package(dir: &Path) -> serde_json::Value {
    26	    std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("package.json")).ok().and_then(|s|serde_json::from_str(&s).ok()).unwrap_or_default()
    27	}
    28	pub(super) fn referenced(dir: &Path, path: &str) -> bool {
    29	    package(dir)["scripts"].as_object().is_some_and(|scripts| scripts.values().any(|v|v.as_str().is_some_and(|s|s.contains(path))))
    30	}
    31	pub(super) fn report(dir: &Path) -> Vec<String> {
    32	    let mut advice = Vec::new();
    33	    for path in ["scripts/frontend-proof.mjs", "scripts/day2-proof.mjs", "scripts/launch.mjs"] {
    34	        if !dir.join(path).exists() && referenced(dir, path) {
    35	            advice.push(format!("info: {path} is missing but package.json still references it; preview `snapback4 init --adapt`, then `snapback4 init --adapt --apply`"));
    36	        }
    37	    }
    38	    if let Ok(text) = std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/day2-proof.mjs")) {
    39	        let hash=digest(&text);
    40	        if let Some(t)=templates().iter().find(|t| t.file=="day2" && t.sha256==hash) {
    41	            if matches!(t.version.as_str(),"0.4.28"|"0.4.29") {
    42	                advice.push(format!("info: scripts/day2-proof.mjs is the unmodified {} starter: it asserts no business rows; preview `snapback4 init --adapt` to replace it and capture current sources/fixtures",t.version));
    43	            }
    44	            if t.version=="0.4.30" || t.version=="0.4.31" {
    45	                let script=package(dir)["scripts"]["day2:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("").to_owned();
    46	                if script=="node scripts/day2-proof.mjs" {
    47	                    advice.push("info: the unmodified day-two starter needs its two saved-source arguments; preview `snapback4 init --adapt`".into());
    48	                }
    49	            }
    50	        }
    51	    }
    52	    if let Ok(text)=std::fs::read_to_string(dir.join("scripts/frontend-proof.mjs")) {
    53	        // Custom JavaScript can hide builds in arbitrary imports/functions. These
    54	        // checks deliberately make no claim about its behavior or ownership.
    55	        let active=text.lines().filter(|line| !line.trim_start().starts_with("//")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join("\n");
    56	        let flat=active.replace(['\"','\'',',','[',']','(',')'], " ");
    57	        let words=flat.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>();
    58	        let serves_dist=words.windows(2).any(|w|w==["startStatic","dist"]);
    59	        let pkg=package(dir);
    60	        let launch=pkg["scripts"]["frontend:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
    61	        let pre=pkg["scripts"]["prefrontend:proof"].as_str().unwrap_or("");
    62	        let builds=|s:&str|s.contains("build")||s.contains("export");
    63	        if serves_dist && !builds(&active) && !builds(launch) && !builds(pre) {
    64	            advice.push("info: scripts/frontend-proof.mjs serves dist with no visible build step (heuristic; imported code may build it); build in the proof or its npm script before startStatic(\"dist\")".into());
    65	        }
    66	        if words.windows(3).any(|w|w==["npx","snapback4","dev"]) {
    67	            advice.push("info: scripts/frontend-proof.mjs launches npx snapback4 dev (heuristic); use startDev from snapback4/proof for an owned server and bounded cleanup".into());
    68	        }
    69	    }
    70	    advice
    71	}

     1	//! The CLI's local service-seat door. This capability never comes from a bearer session.
     2	use super::{Shared, single_header, authority_host, producing_engine, respond_with_cors};

    13	const BODY_LIMIT: usize = 4096;

    16	fn secret_path(store: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    17	    let mut path = store.as_os_str().to_owned();
    18	    path.push("-token");
    19	    path.into()

    22	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — preserve the hardened auxiliary-file checks and the single owner's lifetime.
    23	pub(crate) fn publish(store: &Path) -> io::Result<Authority> {
    24	    #[cfg(unix)] {
    25	        let secret = protocol::hex(&protocol::random()?);
    26	        let mut file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), true, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
    27	        file.set_len(0)?;
    28	        file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0))?;
    29	        file.write_all(secret.as_bytes())?;
    30	        Ok(Authority::new(secret, protocol::store_identity(store)?))
    31	    }
    32	    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
    33	        let _ = store;
    34	        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    35	    }
    36	}
    37	
    38	fn read_secret(store: &Path) -> io::Result<String> {
    39	    #[cfg(unix)] {
    40	        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    41	        let file = snapback4_core::private_fs::auxiliary_file(store, &secret_path(store), false, SECRET_LEN as u64)?;
    42	        if file.metadata()?.permissions().mode() & 0o777 != 0o600 {
    43	            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof must have mode 0600"));
    44	        }
    45	        let mut secret = String::new();
    46	        file.take((SECRET_LEN + 1) as u64).read_to_string(&mut secret)?;
    47	        if secret.len() != SECRET_LEN || !secret.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_hexdigit()) {
    48	            return Err(io::Error::other("the store token proof is incomplete"));
    49	        }
    50	        Ok(secret)
    51	    }
    52	    #[cfg(not(unix))] {
    53	        let _ = store;
    54	        Err(io::Error::other("local service minting requires Unix private files"))
    55	    }
    56	}

    58	fn refusal(code: &str, family: &str, message: &str, rewrite: &str) -> Refusal {
    59	    Refusal::new(code, family, message).site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(rewrite).guide("services")
    60	}

    62	pub(super) fn denied(request: Request) {
    63	    respond_with_cors(request, 403, json!({"denied": refusal("E_AUTH", "auth",
    64	        "service token minting requires a loopback peer and this server's private store proof",
    65	        "run snapback4 token --service <name> on the server host as the store owner (add --release <dir> for serve)")}), false);
    66	}

    68	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — authenticate both ends before minting; the store key never leaves its host file/memory.
    69	pub(super) fn handle(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared) {
    70	    let local = request.remote_addr().is_some_and(|peer| peer.ip().is_loopback())
    71	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "host"), Ok(Some(host)) if authority_host(host)
    72	            .is_some_and(|host| host.parse::<std::net::IpAddr>().is_ok_and(|ip| ip.is_loopback())))
    73	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "origin"), Ok(None))
    74	        && matches!(single_header(&request, "x-snapback-store-secret"), Ok(None));
    75	    let Some(authority) = shared.owner.as_ref().and_then(|o| o.token_authority.as_ref()).filter(|_| local) else {
    76	        return denied(request);
    77	    };
    78	    if request.method() != &Method::Post || request.url() != ENDPOINT
    79	        || request.body_length().is_some_and(|n| n > BODY_LIMIT) { return denied(request); }
    80	    let client = match single_header(&request, CLIENT) {
    81	        Ok(Some(value)) => match protocol::nonce(value) { Some(nonce) => nonce, None => return denied(request) },
    82	        _ => return denied(request),
    83	    };
    84	    let (server, proof) = match (single_header(&request, SERVER), single_header(&request, PROOF)) {
    85	        (Ok(None), Ok(None)) => (None, None),
    86	        (Ok(Some(server)), Ok(Some(proof))) => match protocol::nonce(server) {
    87	            Some(server) => (Some(server), Some(proof.to_owned())), None => return denied(request),
    88	        },
    89	        _ => return denied(request),
    90	    };
    91	    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    92	    if request.as_reader().take((BODY_LIMIT + 1) as u64).read_to_end(&mut bytes).is_err() || bytes.len() > BODY_LIMIT {
    93	        return denied(request);
    94	    }
    95	    let mint: Mint = match serde_json::from_slice::<Mint>(&bytes) {
    96	        Ok(mint) if mint.store == authority.store => mint,
    97	        _ => return denied(request),
    98	    };
    99	    let binding = mint.binding();
   100	    let Some(server) = server else {
   101	        let Some(server) = authority.challenge(client, binding.clone(), std::time::Instant::now()) else { return denied(request); };
   102	        return respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"server_nonce": protocol::hex(&server),
   103	            "server_proof": protocol::server_proof(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding)}), false);
   104	    };
   105	    if !authority.consume(&client, &server, &binding, proof.as_deref().unwrap(), std::time::Instant::now()) {
   106	        return denied(request);
   107	    }
   108	    let result = {
   109	        // Use the same writer and identity routine as offline minting. Sample
   110	        // wall time as the offline CLI does, independent of dev's test clock.
   111	        let engine = producing_engine(shared);
   112	        shared.owner.as_ref().expect("authorized owner").path().and_then(|_| {
   113	            identity::service_session(engine.store(), &mint.service, snapback4_core::now_ms(), mint.days)
   114	        })
   115	    };
   116	    let reply = match result {
   117	        Ok(session) => json!({"session": session}),
   118	        Err(error) => {
   119	            let why = error.refusal().site(ENDPOINT).rewrite(if matches!(error, snapback4_core::RuntimeError::Auth(_)) {
   120	                "use --service with 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -"
   121	            } else { "inspect the store error, repair the store access, then rerun snapback4 token" }).guide("services");
   122	            json!({"denied": why})
   123	        }
   124	    };
   125	    let Some(sealed) = protocol::seal(&authority.secret, &client, &server, &binding, &serde_json::to_vec(&reply).unwrap()) else {
   126	        return denied(request);
   127	    };
   128	    respond_with_cors(request, 200, json!({"sealed": sealed}), false);
   129	}
   130	
   131	fn exchange(address: std::net::SocketAddr, headers: &str, body: &str) -> io::Result<Json> {
   132	    let mut stream = TcpStream::connect_timeout(&address, Duration::from_secs(1))?;
   133	    stream.set_read_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(10)))?;
   134	    stream.set_write_timeout(Some(Duration::from_secs(2)))?;
   135	    // HTTP/1.0 keeps the bounded reply length-delimited. No proxy, DNS or redirects.
   136	    write!(stream, "POST {ENDPOINT} HTTP/1.0\r\nHost: {address}\r\n{headers}Content-Type: application/json\r\nConnection: close\r\nContent-Length: {}\r\n\r\n{body}", body.len())?;
   137	    let mut response = String::new();
   138	    stream.take(8193).read_to_string(&mut response)?;
   139	    response.split_once("\r\n\r\n").filter(|_| response.len() <= 8192
   140	        && (response.starts_with("HTTP/1.0 200 ") || response.starts_with("HTTP/1.1 200 ")))
   141	        .and_then(|(_, body)| serde_json::from_str(body).ok())
   142	        .ok_or_else(|| io::Error::other("invalid local mint reply"))
   143	}

   145	// @ref LLP 3000.019#3.4 — busy token commands ask the announced loopback owner, never open a second writer.
   146	pub(crate) fn mint_live(path: &Path, name: &str, days: i64) -> Result<Session, (i32, String)> {
   147	    let store = path.canonicalize().unwrap_or_else(|_| path.to_owned());
   148	    let owner = crate::store_owner::address(&store).unwrap_or_else(|| "another CLI command or a server starting up (address pending)".into());
   149	    let unavailable = |reason: &str| (2, format!("E_STORE_BUSY at {}: owned by {owner}; {reason}. Restore access to that server's loopback listener and private store proof, then rerun `snapback4 token --service <name>` (with the same --release <dir> for serve). For an older or unreachable server, stop it, rerun token offline, then restart it; for another CLI, wait for it to finish. Leave <database>-owner in place", path.display()));
   150	    let address = crate::store_owner::inspector_address(&store).ok_or_else(|| unavailable("no published loopback listener; no mint request sent"))?;
   151	    let secret = read_secret(&store).map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot read the 0600 store token proof; no mint request sent"))?;
   152	    let mint = Mint { service: name.into(), days, store: protocol::store_identity(&store)
   153	        .map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot identify the owned store; no mint request sent"))? };
   154	    let body = serde_json::to_string(&mint).unwrap();
   155	    if body.len() > BODY_LIMIT { return Err((1, "token: E_AUTH at --service: a service name is 1–64 letters, digits, _ or -; use --service worker".into())); }
   156	    let binding = mint.binding();
   157	    let client = protocol::random().map_err(|_| unavailable("cannot create a fresh challenge; no mint request sent"))?;
   158	    let headers = format!("{CLIENT}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&client));
   159	    let challenge = exchange(address, &headers, &body).map_err(|_| unavailable("server challenge unavailable; no mint proof sent"))?;
   160	    let server = challenge["server_nonce"].as_str().and_then(protocol::nonce)
   161	        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("invalid server challenge; no mint proof sent"))?;
   162	    if !challenge["server_proof"].as_str().is_some_and(|proof| protocol::verify_server(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, proof)) {
   163	        return Err(unavailable("server proof did not verify; no mint proof sent"));
   164	    }
   165	    // Only a verified server receives a client proof. Neither message contains the key.
   166	    let headers = format!("{headers}{SERVER}: {}\r\n{PROOF}: {}\r\n", protocol::hex(&server),
   167	        protocol::client_proof(&secret, &client, &server, &binding));
   168	    let reply = exchange(address, &headers, &body)
   169	        .map_err(|_| unavailable("mint reply unavailable; a session may have been minted, but no token was received"))?;
   170	    let reply: Json = reply["sealed"].as_str().and_then(|sealed| protocol::open(&secret, &client, &server, &binding, sealed))
   171	        .and_then(|bytes| serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).ok())
   172	        .ok_or_else(|| unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"))?;
   173	    if let Some(denied) = reply.get("denied") {
   174	        if let Ok(why) = serde_json::from_value::<Refusal>(denied.clone()) {
   175	            return Err((1, format!("token: {} at {ENDPOINT}: {}; {}", why.code, why.message, why.rewrite.unwrap_or_else(|| "rerun snapback4 token with valid service arguments".into()))));
   176	        }
   177	        return Err(unavailable("server refused the local mint request; restart it with this CLI and retry"));
   178	    }
   179	    let session = &reply["session"];
   180	    let principal = format!("service:{name}");
   181	    let token = session["token"].as_str().filter(|t| t.len() == 43 && t.bytes().all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || b"_-".contains(&c)));
   182	    if session["principal"] != principal || session["kind"] != "service" || token.is_none() || session["expires_at"].as_i64().is_none() {
   183	        return Err(unavailable("server returned an invalid mint reply; a session may have been minted"));
   184	    }
   185	    Ok(Session { scope: snapback4_core::identity::SessionScope::Full, cap_expires_at: None, principal, kind: "service".into(), token: token.unwrap().into(), expires_at: session["expires_at"].as_i64().unwrap() })
   186	}

   123	fn status(

   141	            },
   142	        ),
   143	    }

    30	pub(super) fn declared(shared: &Shared, url: &str) -> Option<(String, RouteSpec)> {
    31	    lock(&shared.bound).schema.routes.iter().find(|(_, route)| route.path == url).map(|(name, route)| (name.clone(), route.clone()))
    32	}

    34	fn refuse(request: Request, status: u16, code: &'static str, message: &str) {
    35	    let mut why = Refusal::new(code, "route", message).guide("routes");
    36	    why.retryable = status >= 500;
    37	    respond_with_cors(request, status, json!({ "denied": why }), false);
    38	}
    39	fn unavailable(request: Request) {
    40	    refuse(request, 503, "E_ROUTE_PENDING", "the route has no settled response; retry the same body");
    41	}
    42	fn pause(shared: &Shared, deadline: Instant) {
    43	    let seq = lock(&shared.seq);
    44	    let _ = shared.committed.wait_timeout(seq, deadline.saturating_duration_since(Instant::now()).min(Duration::from_millis(100))).unwrap_or_else(|p| p.into_inner());
    45	}

    47	/// Bearer sessions are valid on declared public routes even in dev. Personas
    48	/// still pass through the full loopback Host/Origin/peer checks.
    49	fn principal(request: &Request, shared: &Shared) -> Result<String, Refusal> {
    50	    if !matches!(single_header(request, "x-snapback-persona"), Ok(None)) { return principal_of(request, shared).map(|c| c.0); }
    51	    let token = crate::auth::bearer_token(request).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?;
    52	    let engine = lock(&shared.engine);
    53	    snapback4_core::identity::resolve_supervised(engine.store(), &token, snapback4_core::identity::ServiceSample { now: shared.now(), supervisor_revision: shared.worker_revision.load(Ordering::Acquire) }).map_err(|e| e.refusal())?
    54	        .map(|(principal, _, _)| principal).ok_or_else(|| snapback4_core::identity::diagnostics::refused(engine.store(), &token, shared.now(), trusted_dev_request(request, shared)))
    55	}

    57	// @ref LLP 3000.009#5.1 — every provider delivery verifies before durable admission or result collection.
    58	pub(super) fn ingress(mut request: Request, shared: &Shared, name: &str, route: RouteSpec) {
    59	    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_millis(route.wait_ms);
    60	    let deadline_ms = shared.now().saturating_add(route.wait_ms as i64);
    61	    let contract = route.contract(name);
    62	    if route.service.is_none() && let Err(why) = principal(&request, shared) {
    63	        return respond_with_cors(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why }), false);
    64	    }
    65	    // A persona is never a provider credential, even on loopback.
    66	    if route.service.is_some() && !matches!(single_header(&request, "x-snapback-persona"), Ok(None)) {
    67	        return refuse(request, 401, "E_ROUTE_VERIFY", "a provider route requires its signature, not a development persona");
    68	    }
    69	    let mut envelope = RouteRequest { method: "POST".into(), path: route.path.clone(),
    70	        headers: request.headers().iter().map(|h| (h.field.as_str().to_string(), h.value.as_str().to_owned())).collect(), body_hex: String::new() };
    71	    if envelope.validate(&route).is_err() { return refuse(request, 431, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "route headers exceed their bound or are invalid"); }
    72	    let expected = request.body_length();
    73	    if expected.is_some_and(|n| n > BODY_BYTES) { return refuse(request, 413, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "a route body exceeds 64 KiB"); }
    74	    let mut bytes = Vec::new();
    75	    let mut buffer = [0; 8192];
    76	    loop {
    77	        let width = buffer.len().min(BODY_BYTES - bytes.len() + 1);
    78	        match request.as_reader().read(&mut buffer[..width]) {
    79	            Ok(0) => break,
    80	            Ok(n) if n > BODY_BYTES - bytes.len() => return refuse(request, 413, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "a route body exceeds 64 KiB"),
    81	            Ok(n) => bytes.extend_from_slice(&buffer[..n]),
    82	            Err(e) if e.kind() == io::ErrorKind::Interrupted => continue,
    83	            Err(_) => return refuse(request, 400, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "the route body could not be read"),
    84	        }
    85	    }
    86	    if expected.is_some_and(|n| n != bytes.len()) { return refuse(request, 400, "E_ROUTE_INPUT", "the route body ended before Content-Length"); }
    87	    envelope.body_hex = encode_bytes_hex(&bytes);
    88	    drop(bytes);
    89	    if Instant::now() >= deadline || shared.now() >= deadline_ms { return unavailable(request); }
    90	    let (mut run, principal) = if route.service.is_some() {
    91	        let authority = {let engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.route_service_generation(name, &contract)};
    92	        let generation = match authority {
    93	            Ok((_, generation)) => generation, Err(_) => return unavailable(request),
    94	        };
    95	        let id = match verification_token() { Ok(id) => id, Err(_) => return unavailable(request) };
    96	        {
    97	            let mut tickets = lock(&shared.route_verification.tickets);
    98	            if tickets.len() >= ROUTE_WORKERS { drop(tickets); return unavailable(request); }
    99	            tickets.insert(id.clone(), Ticket { name: name.into(), contract: contract.clone(), generation, envelope,
   100	                deadline, deadline_ms, lease: None, outcome: None });
   101	        }
   102	        let guard = TicketGuard { registry: &shared.route_verification, id };
   103	        shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
   104	        let (id, principal) = loop {
   105	            let outcome = lock(&shared.route_verification.tickets).get(&guard.id).and_then(|ticket| ticket.outcome.clone());
   106	            match outcome {
   107	                Some(Verified::Allowed { id, principal }) => break (id, principal),
   108	                Some(Verified::Denied) => return refuse(request, 403, "E_ROUTE_VERIFY", "the provider signature was refused"),
   109	                Some(Verified::Unavailable) => return unavailable(request),
   110	                None if Instant::now() >= deadline || shared.now() >= deadline_ms || crate::lifecycle::stopping() => return unavailable(request),
   111	                None => pause(shared, deadline),
   112	            }
   113	        };
   114	        drop(guard);
   115	        let collected = {let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.route_request_at(name, &contract, &id, &principal, shared.now())};
   116	        match collected { Ok(run) => (run, principal), Err(_) => return unavailable(request) }
   117	    } else {
   118	        let principal = match principal(&request, shared) { Ok(p) => p, Err(why) => return respond_with_cors(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why }), false) };
   119	        let mut context = Context::new(&principal, BTreeMap::new()).at(shared.now());
   120	        if let Ok(token) = crate::auth::bearer_token(&request) { context = context.with_session(token); }
   121	        let admitted = {
   122	            let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);
   123	            if let Err(why) = super::session_identity::mutation_authority(&request, shared, &engine, &principal) {
   124	                drop(engine); return respond_with_cors(request,401,json!({"denied":why}),false);
   125	            }
   126	            crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());
   127	            engine.request_session_route(name, &contract, &envelope, context)
   128	        };
   129	        match admitted { Ok((run, _)) => (run, principal), Err(error) => return respond_with_cors(request, if error.refusal().family == "auth" { 401 } else { 503 }, json!({ "denied": error.refusal() }), false) }
   130	    };
   131	    shared.durable_committed(&lock(&shared.engine));
   132	    loop {
   133	        if route.service.is_none() && let Err(why) = self::principal(&request, shared) {
   134	            return respond_with_cors(request, 401, json!({ "denied": why }), false);
   135	        }
   136	        // Recheck even an immediately settled replay: revocation and adoption
   137	        // must not turn a retained response into a new authority grant.
   138	        let collected = {let mut engine=lock(&shared.engine);crate::release_requests::stamp(engine.generation());engine.route_request_at(name, &contract, &run.id, &principal, shared.now())};
   139	        run = match collected {
   140	            Ok(run) => run, Err(_) => return unavailable(request),
   141	        };
   142	        if run.state == "done" { return completed(request, &run); }
   143	        if matches!(run.state.as_str(), "failed" | "parked") || Instant::now() >= deadline || shared.now() >= deadline_ms || crate::lifecycle::stopping() { return unavailable(request); }
   144	        pause(shared, deadline);
   145	    }

+import subprocess,pathlib,json,urllib.request,urllib.error,os,select,time,signal,sys
+base=pathlib.Path('/tmp/sb4-stage-repro');lane=next(base.glob(sys.argv[1]+'-*'))
+# Only this recorded directly spawned npm wrapper receives a shutdown signal.
+log=open('/tmp/stage-repro-'+sys.argv[1]+'.stderr.log','w')
+p=subprocess.Popen(['npm','run','probe:serve'],cwd=lane,stdout=subprocess.PIPE,stderr=log,text=True,bufsize=1,start_new_session=True)
+try:
+ end=time.monotonic()+360;line=None
+ while time.monotonic()<end:
+  if p.poll() is not None:raise RuntimeError('probe died '+str(p.returncode))
+  r,_,_=select.select([p.stdout],[],[],1)
+  if not r:continue
+  candidate=p.stdout.readline()
+  if candidate.startswith('{'):
+   result=json.loads(candidate)
+   if 'backendUrl' in result:line=result;break
+ if line is None:raise RuntimeError('probe readiness timeout')
+ def request(path,body=None,token=None,persona=None):
+  hdr={}
+  if token:hdr['Authorization']='Bearer '+token
+  if persona:hdr['x-snapback-persona']=persona
+  if body is not None:hdr['content-type']='application/json'
+  r=urllib.request.Request(line['backendUrl']+path,data=None if body is None else json.dumps(body).encode(),headers=hdr)
+  try:
+   with urllib.request.urlopen(r,timeout=15) as response:return response.status,json.load(response)
+  except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:return e.code,json.load(e)
+ status,cfg=request('/auth/config');assert status==200 and cfg['development'] is False and cfg['devDoors'] is False and cfg['environment']=='staging',cfg
+ checked={}
+ for role,users in line['sessions'].items():
+  assert len(users)>=2
+  for u in users:
+   status,signed=request('/auth/login',{'email':u['email'],'password':u['password']});assert status==200 and signed['session']['principal']==u['principal']
+   status,cat=request('/',token=signed['session']['token']);assert status==200 and cat['ops']
+  checked[role]=len(users)
+ assert request('/dev/status')[0]==403
+ assert request('/seed',[])[0]==403
+ assert request('/q/myRole',{'args':{}},persona='alice')[0]==401
+ with urllib.request.urlopen(line['frontendUrl'],timeout=15) as response:assert response.status==200
+ evidence={'lane':lane.name,'status':'passed','backend':'serve','development':cfg['development'],'environment':cfg['environment'],'devDoors':cfg['devDoors'],'passwordLogins':checked,'unauthenticatedSeed':403,'unauthenticatedStatus':403,'persona':401,'frontend':200,'aliveAtEnd':p.poll() is None,'credentials':'kept in memory; omitted from evidence'}
+ pathlib.Path('/tmp/stage-repro-'+sys.argv[1]+'.json').write_text(json.dumps(evidence,indent=2)+'\n');print(json.dumps(evidence))
+finally:
+ if p.poll() is None:os.killpg(p.pid,signal.SIGTERM)
+ try:p.wait(timeout=20)
+ except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:

   353	    if !shared.personas { return respond(request, 403, json!({"denied":refusal_json(&crate::dev_clock::release())})); }
